========================================
FILE: supabase/migrations/20261018084709_create_profiles_table.sql
========================================
/*
# Create profiles table for Noor app

1. New Tables
- `profiles` — stores per-user settings for the Noor Islamic companion app
  - `id` (uuid, PK, references auth.users) — one row per authenticated user
  - `display_name` (text) — user's display name shown in the UI
  - `city_id` (text) — selected city ID for prayer time calculation
  - `calculation_method` (text, default 'jafari') — prayer calculation method
  - `notif_enabled` (boolean, default true) — master notification toggle
  - `theme_id` (text, default 'emerald') — selected UI theme
  - `adhan_id` (text) — selected adhan sound
  - `created_at` (timestamptz)
  - `updated_at` (timestamptz)

2. Security
- Enable RLS on `profiles`.
- Owner-scoped CRUD: each authenticated user can only access their own profile row.
- INSERT policy uses WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = id) so only the owner can create their row.
- UPDATE policy uses USING + WITH CHECK so only the owner can modify their row.
- SELECT policy uses USING so only the owner can read their row.
- DELETE policy uses USING so only the owner can delete their row.
*/

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profiles (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  display_name text DEFAULT '',
  city_id text DEFAULT 'tehran',
  calculation_method text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'jafari',
  notif_enabled boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  theme_id text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'emerald',
  adhan_id text DEFAULT 'default',
  created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()
);

ALTER TABLE profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "select_own_profile" ON profiles;
CREATE POLICY "select_own_profile"
  ON profiles FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "insert_own_profile" ON profiles;
CREATE POLICY "insert_own_profile"
  ON profiles FOR INSERT
  TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "update_own_profile" ON profiles;
CREATE POLICY "update_own_profile"
  ON profiles FOR UPDATE
  TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = id)
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "delete_own_profile" ON profiles;
CREATE POLICY "delete_own_profile"
  ON profiles FOR DELETE
  TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = id);


========================================
FILE: supabase/migrations/20261018084723_create_tasbih_counts_table.sql
========================================
/*
# Create tasbih_counts table for Noor app

1. New Tables
- `tasbih_counts` — stores per-user tasbih (dhikr counter) data
  - `id` (uuid, PK)
  - `user_id` (uuid, NOT NULL, DEFAULT auth.uid(), references auth.users) — owner
  - `dhikr_id` (text) — which dhikr was counted
  - `count` (integer, default 0) — current count in session
  - `target` (integer, default 33) — target count for one round
  - `completed` (integer, default 0) — number of completed rounds
  - `date` (date) — the day this count belongs to (local date)
  - `created_at` (timestamptz)
  - `updated_at` (timestamptz)

2. Security
- Enable RLS on `tasbih_counts`.
- Owner-scoped CRUD: each authenticated user can only access their own counts.
- user_id defaults to auth.uid() so inserts that omit user_id succeed.
*/

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasbih_counts (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid() REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  dhikr_id text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'subhanallah',
  count integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  target integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 33,
  completed integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  date date NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
  created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()
);

ALTER TABLE tasbih_counts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "select_own_tasbih" ON tasbih_counts;
CREATE POLICY "select_own_tasbih"
  ON tasbih_counts FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "insert_own_tasbih" ON tasbih_counts;
CREATE POLICY "insert_own_tasbih"
  ON tasbih_counts FOR INSERT
  TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "update_own_tasbih" ON tasbih_counts;
CREATE POLICY "update_own_tasbih"
  ON tasbih_counts FOR UPDATE
  TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id)
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "delete_own_tasbih" ON tasbih_counts;
CREATE POLICY "delete_own_tasbih"
  ON tasbih_counts FOR DELETE
  TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

import { Capacitor, registerPlugin } from '@capacitor/core';

export type Adhan = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  nameFa: string;
  reciter: string;
  originFa: string;
  audioUrl: string;
  bundledOffline?: boolean;
  license?: string;
};

interface NativeAdhanBridge {
  downloadAdhanAudio(opts: { adhanId: string; audioUrl: string }): Promise<{ downloaded: boolean; path?: string }>;
  listDownloadedAdhans(): Promise<{ ids: string[] }>;
  previewAdhanAudio(opts: { adhanId: string; audioUrl: string; durationSeconds?: number; volumePercent?: number }): Promise<void>;
  stopPreviewAudio(): Promise<void>;
}

const NativeAdhan = registerPlugin<NativeAdhanBridge>('NoorAlarm');

const DOWNLOADED_ADHANS_KEY = 'noor_downloaded_adhans_v2';

export function getDownloadedAdhanIds(): string[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(DOWNLOADED_ADHANS_KEY);
    const parsed: string[] = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
    return Array.from(new Set(['mahur_saeed', ...parsed]));
  } catch {
    return ['mahur_saeed'];
  }
}

export async function syncNativeDownloadedAdhans(): Promise<string[]> {
  if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android') {
    try {
      const res = await NativeAdhan.listDownloadedAdhans();
      if (Array.isArray(res.ids)) {
        const merged = Array.from(new Set(['mahur_saeed', ...getDownloadedAdhanIds(), ...res.ids]));
        localStorage.setItem(DOWNLOADED_ADHANS_KEY, JSON.stringify(merged));
        return merged;
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }
  return getDownloadedAdhanIds();
}

export async function downloadAndCacheAdhan(adhan: Adhan): Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }> {
  try {
    if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android') {
      const res = await NativeAdhan.downloadAdhanAudio({
        adhanId: adhan.id,
        audioUrl: adhan.audioUrl,
      });
      if (res.downloaded) {
        const updated = Array.from(new Set([...getDownloadedAdhanIds(), adhan.id]));
        localStorage.setItem(DOWNLOADED_ADHANS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
        return { ok: true };
      }
    } else if ('caches' in window) {
      const cache = await caches.open('noor-adhan-audio-v2');
      await cache.add(new Request(adhan.audioUrl, { mode: 'no-cors' }));
      const updated = Array.from(new Set([...getDownloadedAdhanIds(), adhan.id]));
      localStorage.setItem(DOWNLOADED_ADHANS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
      return { ok: true };
    }
    const updated = Array.from(new Set([...getDownloadedAdhanIds(), adhan.id]));
    localStorage.setItem(DOWNLOADED_ADHANS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در دانلود فایل صوتی؛ اتصال اینترنت را بررسی کنید.',
    };
  }
}

let webAudioPlayer: HTMLAudioElement | null = null;

export async function playAdhanPreview(adhan: Adhan, durationSeconds = 15, volumePercent = 90): Promise<void> {
  if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android') {
    await NativeAdhan.previewAdhanAudio({
      adhanId: adhan.id,
      audioUrl: adhan.audioUrl,
      durationSeconds,
      volumePercent,
    });
    return;
  }
  stopAdhanPreview();
  webAudioPlayer = new Audio(adhan.audioUrl);
  webAudioPlayer.volume = Math.max(0.05, Math.min(1, volumePercent / 100));
  await webAudioPlayer.play();
  if (durationSeconds > 0) {
    window.setTimeout(() => stopAdhanPreview(), durationSeconds * 1000);
  }
}

export function stopAdhanPreview(): void {
  if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android') {
    void NativeAdhan.stopPreviewAudio();
  }
  if (webAudioPlayer) {
    webAudioPlayer.pause();
    webAudioPlayer.currentTime = 0;
    webAudioPlayer = null;
  }
}

const GUEST_CITY_KEY = 'noor_guest_city_id';

export function getCityById(id: string | null | undefined): City {
  if (!id) return CITIES[0];
  return CITIES.find((c) => c.id === id) ?? CITIES[0];
}

export function getGuestCityId(): string {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(GUEST_CITY_KEY) || 'tehran';
  } catch {
    return 'tehran';
  }
}

export type SoundProfileId = 'home' | 'work' | 'travel';
export type PrayerAlarmMode = 'full_adhan' | 'short_10s' | 'short_beep' | 'silent';

export interface PrayerStudioConfig {
  mode: PrayerAlarmMode;
  volumePercent: number; // 10 to 100
  preReminderMinutes: number; // 0, 10, 15, 30
  preDuaText: string;
  adhanId: string;
}

export interface AdhanStudioState {
  activeProfile: SoundProfileId;
  profiles: Record<SoundProfileId, {
    nameFa: string;
    badge: string;
    descriptionFa: string;
    prayers: Record<'fajr' | 'dhuhr' | 'asr' | 'maghrib' | 'isha', PrayerStudioConfig>;
  }>;
}

export interface QuranContinuityState {
  surahIndex: number;
  surahNameFa: string;
  ayahNumber: number;
  totalAyahs: number;
  dailyTargetMinutes: 5 | 15 | 30;
  todayReadMinutes: number;
  lastReadIso: string | null;
  bookmarks: Array<{ surahNameFa: string; ayahNumber: number; note: string }>;
}

export interface MosqueAndTravelState {
  travelModeEnabled: boolean;
  originCityId: string;
  destinationCityId: string;
  isQasrPrayer: boolean;
  selectedMosqueName: string;
  jamaatOffsetsMinutes: {
    fajr: number;
    dhuhr: number;
    maghrib: number;
  };
  mosqueAnnouncement: string;
}

export interface MuhasabaEntry {
  id: string;
  date: string;
  mood: 'peaceful' | 'grateful' | 'hopeful' | 'repentant';
  gratitudeNote: string;
  selfReflection: string;
  tomorrowIntention: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export type RoutineBudgetMinutes = 2 | 5 | 15;

export function loadAdhanStudio(): AdhanStudioState {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STUDIO_KEY);
    if (!raw) return DEFAULT_STUDIO;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return { ...DEFAULT_STUDIO, ...parsed };
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_STUDIO;
  }
}

export function saveAdhanStudio(state: AdhanStudioState): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STUDIO_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export function addMinutesToTimeStr(timeHHMM: string, addMin: number): string {
  const [h, m] = timeHHMM.split(':').map(Number);
  if (isNaN(h) || isNaN(m)) return timeHHMM;
  const total = (h * 60 + m + addMin + 1440) % 1440;
  const nh = Math.floor(total / 60);
  const nm = total % 60;
  return `${String(nh).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(nm).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

export function loadMuhasabaEntries(): MuhasabaEntry[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(MUHASABA_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export function addMuhasabaEntry(entry: Omit<MuhasabaEntry, 'id' | 'createdAt'>): MuhasabaEntry {
  const existing = loadMuhasabaEntries();
  const item: MuhasabaEntry = {
    ...entry,
    id: `m_${Date.now()}`,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
  const updated = [item, ...existing].slice(0, 90);
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(MUHASABA_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
  return item;
}

export function getRoutineBudgetMinutes(): RoutineBudgetMinutes {
  try {
    const v = Number(localStorage.getItem(ROUTINE_BUDGET_KEY));
    if (v === 2 || v === 5 || v === 15) return v;
    return 5;
  } catch {
    return 5;
  }
}

export function setRoutineBudgetMinutes(min: RoutineBudgetMinutes): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(ROUTINE_BUDGET_KEY, String(min));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

const CUSTOM_DHIKR_KEY = 'noor_custom_dhikr_v1';

export function getCustomDhikr(): Dhikr[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CUSTOM_DHIKR_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
    return parsed.filter((item): item is Dhikr =>
      !!item && typeof item.id === 'string' &&
      typeof item.arabic === 'string' && typeof item.translation === 'string' &&
      typeof item.translit === 'string' && typeof item.virtue === 'string' &&
      typeof item.count === 'number' && item.count > 0
    );
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export function getAllDhikr(): Dhikr[] {
  return [...dhikrLibrary, ...getCustomDhikr()];
}

export function addCustomDhikr(input: {
  arabic: string;
  translation: string;
  count: number;
  recommendedTime?: string;
  source?: string;
}): Dhikr {
  const item: Dhikr = {
    id: `custom_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    arabic: input.arabic.trim(),
    translit: 'یادداشت / ذکر شخصی',
    translation: input.translation.trim() || 'ذکر شخصی ثبت‌شده از سخنرانی یا برنامه فردی',
    virtue: 'ثبت‌شده در دفترچه معنوی شما برای مداومت و عمل',
    count: Math.max(1, Math.floor(input.count)),
    category: 'tasbih',
    source: input.source?.trim() || 'دفترچه یادداشت معنوی من',
    recommendedTime: input.recommendedTime?.trim() || 'طبق برنامه روزانه شما',
  };
  const next = [...getCustomDhikr(), item];
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(CUSTOM_DHIKR_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
  } catch {
    throw new Error('ذکر ذخیره نشد؛ فضای ذخیره‌سازی گوشی را بررسی کنید.');
  }
  return item;
}

export function getDhikr(id: string | null | undefined): Dhikr | null {
  if (!id) return null;
  return getAllDhikr().find((d) => d.id === id) ?? null;
}

export const dhikrCategories: Record<string, string> = {
  scholars: 'توصیه علما',
  mafatih: 'مفاتیح و زیارات',
  quran: 'قرآن و سوره‌ها',
  tasbih: 'تسبیح',
  salawat: 'صلوات',
  istighfar: 'استغفار',
  tahlil: 'تهلیل و توکل',
  takbir: 'تکبیر',
};

FILE: src/lib/jalali.ts

const jalaliMonths = [
  'فروردین', 'اردیبهشت', 'خرداد', 'تیر', 'مرداد', 'شهریور',
  'مهر', 'آبان', 'آذر', 'دی', 'بهمن', 'اسفند',
];

const jalaliWeekdays = ['یکشنبه', 'دوشنبه', 'سه‌شنبه', 'چهارشنبه', 'پنجشنبه', 'جمعه', 'شنبه'];

function div(a: number, b: number): number { return Math.floor(a / b); }
function mod(a: number, b: number): number { return a - Math.floor(a / b) * b; }

function gregorianToJalali(gy: number, gm: number, gd: number): [number, number, number] {
  const g_d_m = [0, 31, 59, 90, 120, 151, 181, 212, 243, 273, 304, 334];
  let jy = (gy <= 1600) ? 0 : 979;
  gy -= (gy <= 1600) ? 621 : 1600;
  const gy2 = (gm > 2) ? (gy + 1) : gy;

  // Correct Gregorian-to-Jalali epoch offset and leap-year accounting.
  let days = (365 * gy)
    + div(gy2 + 3, 4)
    - div(gy2 + 99, 100)
    + div(gy2 + 399, 400)
    - 80
    + gd
    + g_d_m[gm - 1];

  jy += 33 * div(days, 12053);
  days = mod(days, 12053);
  jy += 4 * div(days, 1461);
  days = mod(days, 1461);
  if (days > 365) {
    jy += div((days - 1), 365);
    days = mod((days - 1), 365);
  }

  let jm: number;
  if (days < 186) {
    jm = 1 + div(days, 31);
    days = 1 + mod(days, 31);
  } else {
    days -= 186;
    jm = 7 + div(days, 30);
    days = 1 + mod(days, 30);
  }
  return [jy, jm, days];
}

const faDigits = ['۰', '۱', '۲', '۳', '۴', '۵', '۶', '۷', '۸', '۹'];

function toFaDigits(s: string | number): string {
  return String(s).replace(/[0-9]/g, (d) => faDigits[parseInt(d, 10)]);
}

export function toJalaliShort(date: Date): string {
  const [jy, jm, jd] = gregorianToJalali(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth() + 1, date.getDate());
  return `${toFaDigits(jd)} ${jalaliMonths[jm - 1]} ${toFaDigits(jy)}`;
}

export function toJalaliFull(date: Date): string {
  const [jy, jm, jd] = gregorianToJalali(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth() + 1, date.getDate());
  return `${toFaDigits(jy)}/${toFaDigits(String(jm).padStart(2, '0'))}/${toFaDigits(String(jd).padStart(2, '0'))}`;
}

export function getJalaliWeekday(date: Date): string {
  return jalaliWeekdays[date.getDay()];
}

export function getJalaliMonthDay(date: Date): string {
  const [, jm, jd] = gregorianToJalali(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth() + 1, date.getDate());
  return `${toFaDigits(jd)} ${jalaliMonths[jm - 1]}`;
}

export { jalaliMonths, jalaliWeekdays, toFaDigits };


export function jalaliToGregorian(jy: number, jm: number, jd: number): [number, number, number] {
  jy += 1595;
  let days = -355668 + (365 * jy) + (Math.floor(jy / 33) * 8) + Math.floor(((jy % 33) + 3) / 4) + jd + ((jm < 7) ? (jm - 1) * 31 : ((jm - 7) * 30) + 186);
  let gy = 400 * Math.floor(days / 146097);
  days %= 146097;
  if (days > 36524) {
    gy += 100 * Math.floor(--days / 36524);
    days %= 36524;
    if (days >= 365) days++;
  }
  gy += 4 * Math.floor(days / 1461);
  days %= 1461;
  if (days > 365) {
    gy += Math.floor((days - 1) / 365);
    days = (days - 1) % 365;
  }
  let gd = days + 1;
  const sal_a = [0, 31, ((gy % 4 === 0 && gy % 100 !== 0) || (gy % 400 === 0)) ? 29 : 28, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31];
  let gm = 0;
  for (gm = 0; gm < 13 && gd > sal_a[gm]; gm++) gd -= sal_a[gm];
  return [gy, gm, gd];
}

export function getJalaliParts(date: Date): { jy: number; jm: number; jd: number } {
  const [jy, jm, jd] = gregorianToJalali(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth() + 1, date.getDate());
  return { jy, jm, jd };
}

export type LogLevel = 'info' | 'warn' | 'error';

export interface AppLogEntry {
  timestamp: string;
  level: LogLevel;
  scope: string;
  message: string;
  meta?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

const LOG_STORAGE_KEY = 'noor_diagnostic_logs_v1';
const MAX_LOGS = 100;

export function logEvent(
  level: LogLevel,
  scope: string,
  message: string,
  meta?: Record<string, unknown>
): void {
  const entry: AppLogEntry = {
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    level,
    scope,
    message,
    meta,
  };

  const formatted = `[Noor][${entry.timestamp}][${level.toUpperCase()}][${scope}] ${message}`;
  if (level === 'error') console.error(formatted, meta ?? '');
  else if (level === 'warn') console.warn(formatted, meta ?? '');
  else console.info(formatted, meta ?? '');

  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(LOG_STORAGE_KEY);
    const existing: AppLogEntry[] = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
    localStorage.setItem(LOG_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify([entry, ...existing].slice(0, MAX_LOGS)));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export function getDiagnosticLogs(): AppLogEntry[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(LOG_STORAGE_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export function clearDiagnosticLogs(): void {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(LOG_STORAGE_KEY);
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export type PrayerTime = {
  name: string;
  nameFa: string;
  time: string;
  key: string;
};

export type PrayerTimes = {
  fajr: string;
  sunrise: string;
  dhuhr: string;
  asr: string;
  maghrib: string;
  isha: string;
  midnight: string;
  date: string;
  hijri: string;
  hijriDate: string;
  location: string;
};

const prayerNames: PrayerTime[] = [
  { name: 'Fajr', nameFa: 'صبح', time: '', key: 'fajr' },
  { name: 'Sunrise', nameFa: 'طلوع', time: '', key: 'sunrise' },
  { name: 'Dhuhr', nameFa: 'ظهر', time: '', key: 'dhuhr' },
  { name: 'Asr', nameFa: 'عصر', time: '', key: 'asr' },
  { name: 'Maghrib', nameFa: 'مغرب', time: '', key: 'maghrib' },
  { name: 'Isha', nameFa: 'عشاء', time: '', key: 'isha' },
  { name: 'Midnight', nameFa: 'نیمه‌شب', time: '', key: 'midnight' },
];

export function getPrayerNames(): PrayerTime[] {
  return prayerNames;
}

function julianDate(year: number, month: number, day: number): number {
  if (month <= 2) {
    year -= 1;
    month += 12;
  }
  const A = Math.floor(year / 100);
  const B = 2 - A + Math.floor(A / 4);
  return Math.floor(365.25 * (year + 4716)) + Math.floor(30.6001 * (month + 1)) + day + B - 1524.5;
}

function gregorianToHijri(year: number, month: number, day: number): { year: number; month: number; day: number } {
  const jd = julianDate(year, month, day);
  const l1 = jd - 1948440 + 10632;
  const n = Math.floor((l1 - 1) / 10631);
  const l2 = l1 - 10631 * n + 354;
  const j =
    Math.floor((10985 - l2) / 5316) * Math.floor((50 * l2) / 17719) +
    Math.floor(l2 / 5670) * Math.floor((43 * l2) / 15238);
  const l3 = l2 - Math.floor((30 - j) / 15) * Math.floor((17719 * j) / 50) -
    Math.floor(j / 16) * Math.floor((15238 * j) / 43) + 29;
  const m = Math.floor((24 * l3) / 709);
  const d = l3 - Math.floor(709 * m / 24);
  const y = 30 * n + j - 30;
  return { year: y, month: m, day: d };
}

const hijriMonthsFa = [
  'محرم', 'صفر', 'ربیع‌الاول', 'ربیع‌الثانی', 'جمادی‌الاول', 'جمادی‌الثانی',
  'رجب', 'شعبان', 'رمضان', 'شوال', 'ذی‌القعده', 'ذی‌الحجه',
];

function deg2rad(d: number): number { return (d * Math.PI) / 180; }
function rad2deg(r: number): number { return (r * 180) / Math.PI; }

export function timeToMinutes(t: string): number {
  const [h, m] = t.split(':').map(Number);
  if (isNaN(h) || isNaN(m)) return NaN;
  return h * 60 + m;
}

export function minutesToTime(min: number): string {
  if (min < 0) min += 24 * 60;
  if (min >= 24 * 60) min -= 24 * 60;
  const h = Math.floor(min / 60);
  const m = Math.floor(min % 60);
  return `${String(h).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

export function getTimezoneOffsetHours(timezone: string, date: Date = new Date()): number {
  try {
    const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
      timeZone: timezone,
      hour12: false,
      year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit',
      hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit',
    });
    const parts = dtf.formatToParts(date);
    const map: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (const p of parts) {
      if (p.type !== 'literal') map[p.type] = parseInt(p.value, 10);
    }
    const asUTC = Date.UTC(map.year, map.month - 1, map.day, map.hour % 24, map.minute, map.second);
    return (asUTC - date.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  } catch {
    return -date.getTimezoneOffset() / 60;
  }
}

export function getLocalDateString(date: Date = new Date(), timezone?: string): string {
  if (timezone) {
    try {
      const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
        timeZone: timezone,
        year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit',
      });
      return dtf.format(date);
    } catch {
      // fall through
    }
  }
  const d = date;
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

type MethodParams = { fajrAngle: number; ishaAngle: number; maghribAngle?: number; ishaMinutesAfterMaghrib?: number };

const METHODS: Record<string, MethodParams> = {
  jafari: { fajrAngle: 16, ishaAngle: 14, maghribAngle: 4 },
  tehran: { fajrAngle: 17.7, ishaAngle: 14, maghribAngle: 4.5 },
  mwl: { fajrAngle: 18, ishaAngle: 17 },
  egyptian: { fajrAngle: 19.5, ishaAngle: 17.5 },
  karachi: { fajrAngle: 18, ishaAngle: 18 },
  umm_al_qura: { fajrAngle: 18.5, ishaAngle: 0, ishaMinutesAfterMaghrib: 90 },
};

export function getAvailableMethods(): { id: string; name: string }[] {
  return [
    { id: 'jafari', name: 'جعفری (شیعه)' },
    { id: 'tehran', name: 'تهران (ژئوفیزیک)' },
    { id: 'mwl', name: 'اتحادیه اسلامی جهانی' },
    { id: 'egyptian', name: 'مصری' },
    { id: 'karachi', name: 'کراچی' },
    { id: 'umm_al_qura', name: 'ام‌القری' },
  ];
}

export function calculatePrayerTimes(
  date: Date,
  lat: number,
  lng: number,
  timezone: number | string = 'Asia/Tehran',
  method: string = 'jafari',
): PrayerTimes {
  const tzOffset = typeof timezone === 'string'
    ? getTimezoneOffsetHours(timezone, date)
    : timezone;

  const params = METHODS[method] ?? METHODS.jafari;

  const tzName = typeof timezone === 'string' ? timezone : undefined;
  const localDateStr = getLocalDateString(date, tzName);
  const [calcYear, calcMonth, calcDay] = localDateStr.split('-').map(Number);
  const calcDate = new Date(calcYear, calcMonth - 1, calcDay);

  const j = julianDate(calcDate.getFullYear(), calcDate.getMonth() + 1, calcDate.getDate()) - 2451545;

  const sunLng = normalizeDeg(280.46 + 0.9856474 * j);
  const M = normalizeDeg(357.528 + 0.9856003 * j);
  const ec = 1.915 * Math.sin(deg2rad(M)) + 0.02 * Math.sin(deg2rad(2 * M));
  const trueLng = sunLng + ec;
  const omega = normalizeDeg(125.04 - 0.052954 * j);
  const lambda = trueLng - 0.00569 - 0.00478 * Math.sin(deg2rad(omega));

  const declination = rad2deg(Math.asin(
    Math.sin(deg2rad(23.44)) * Math.sin(deg2rad(lambda))
  ));

  const dayOfYear = Math.floor(
    (Date.UTC(calcDate.getFullYear(), calcDate.getMonth(), calcDate.getDate()) -
      Date.UTC(calcDate.getFullYear(), 0, 0)) / 86400000
  );
  const B = deg2rad(360 * (dayOfYear - 81) / 364);
  const eotMin = 9.87 * Math.sin(2 * B) - 7.53 * Math.cos(B) - 1.5 * Math.sin(B);

  const tzMeridian = tzOffset * 15;
  const solarNoonMin = 720 + 4 * (tzMeridian - lng) - eotMin;
  const noonMinutes = solarNoonMin;

  function sunAngleTime(angle: number, isRising: boolean, baseMin: number): string {
    const latRad = deg2rad(lat);
    const decRad = deg2rad(declination);
    const cosH = (-Math.sin(deg2rad(angle)) - Math.sin(latRad) * Math.sin(decRad)) /
      (Math.cos(latRad) * Math.cos(decRad));
    if (cosH < -1 || cosH > 1) return '--:--';
    const H = rad2deg(Math.acos(cosH));
    const hourAngleMin = (isRising ? -H : H) / 15 * 60;
    return minutesToTime(baseMin + hourAngleMin);
  }

  function asrTime(factor: number, baseMin: number): string {
    const latRad = deg2rad(lat);
    const decRad = deg2rad(declination);
    const tanAlpha = 1 / (factor + Math.tan(Math.abs(latRad - decRad)));
    const alphaRad = Math.atan(tanAlpha);
    const sinAlpha = Math.sin(alphaRad);
    const cosH = (sinAlpha - Math.sin(latRad) * Math.sin(decRad)) /
      (Math.cos(latRad) * Math.cos(decRad));
    if (cosH < -1 || cosH > 1) return '--:--';
    const H = rad2deg(Math.acos(cosH));
    return minutesToTime(baseMin + H / 15 * 60);
  }

  const fajr = sunAngleTime(params.fajrAngle, true, noonMinutes);
  const sunrise = sunAngleTime(0.833, true, noonMinutes);
  const sunset = sunAngleTime(0.833, false, noonMinutes);
  const dhuhr = minutesToTime(noonMinutes + 2);
  const asr = asrTime(1, noonMinutes);
  const maghribAngle = params.maghribAngle ?? 0.833;
  const maghrib = sunAngleTime(maghribAngle, false, noonMinutes);

  let isha: string;
  if (params.ishaMinutesAfterMaghrib) {
    isha = minutesToTime(timeToMinutes(maghrib) + params.ishaMinutesAfterMaghrib);
  } else {
    isha = sunAngleTime(params.ishaAngle, false, noonMinutes);
  }

  const sunsetMin = timeToMinutes(sunset);
  const sunriseMin = timeToMinutes(sunrise);
  let midnightMin: number;
  if (!isNaN(sunsetMin) && !isNaN(sunriseMin)) {
    const nextSunriseMin = sunriseMin + 24 * 60;
    midnightMin = (sunsetMin + nextSunriseMin) / 2;
    if (midnightMin >= 24 * 60) midnightMin -= 24 * 60;
  } else {
    midnightMin = 0;
  }
  const midnight = minutesToTime(midnightMin);

  const hijri = gregorianToHijri(calcDate.getFullYear(), calcDate.getMonth() + 1, calcDate.getDate());
  const hijriDate = `${hijri.day} ${hijriMonthsFa[hijri.month - 1]} ${hijri.year}`;

  const dateStr = localDateStr;

  return {
    fajr,
    sunrise,
    dhuhr,
    asr,
    maghrib,
    isha,
    midnight,
    date: dateStr,
    hijri: `${hijri.year}/${String(hijri.month).padStart(2, '0')}/${String(hijri.day).padStart(2, '0')}`,
    hijriDate,
    location: '',
  };
}

function normalizeDeg(deg: number): number {
  deg = deg % 360;
  if (deg < 0) deg += 360;
  return deg;
}

export function getNextPrayer(
  times: PrayerTimes,
  timezone?: string,
): { name: string; nameFa: string; time: string; key: string; remaining: string } | null {
  const now = new Date();
  let nowMin: number;
  if (timezone) {
    const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
      timeZone: timezone,
      hour12: false,
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
    });
    const parts = dtf.formatToParts(now);
    const h = parseInt(parts.find((p) => p.type === 'hour')?.value ?? '0', 10);
    const m = parseInt(parts.find((p) => p.type === 'minute')?.value ?? '0', 10);
    nowMin = (h % 24) * 60 + m;
  } else {
    nowMin = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
  }
  const prayers = [
    { name: 'Fajr', nameFa: 'صبح', time: times.fajr, key: 'fajr' },
    { name: 'Dhuhr', nameFa: 'ظهر', time: times.dhuhr, key: 'dhuhr' },
    { name: 'Asr', nameFa: 'عصر', time: times.asr, key: 'asr' },
    { name: 'Maghrib', nameFa: 'مغرب', time: times.maghrib, key: 'maghrib' },
    { name: 'Isha', nameFa: 'عشاء', time: times.isha, key: 'isha' },
  ];
  for (const p of prayers) {
    const pMin = timeToMinutes(p.time);
    if (isNaN(pMin)) continue;
    if (pMin > nowMin) {
      const diff = pMin - nowMin;
      const hours = Math.floor(diff / 60);
      const mins = diff % 60;
      return { ...p, remaining: `${hours.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}س ${mins.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}د` };
    }
  }
  const fMin = timeToMinutes(times.fajr);
  if (isNaN(fMin)) return null;
  const nextFajrMin = fMin + 24 * 60;
  const diff = nextFajrMin - nowMin;
  const hours = Math.floor(diff / 60);
  const mins = diff % 60;
  return { name: 'Fajr', nameFa: 'صبح', time: times.fajr, key: 'fajr', remaining: `${hours.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}س ${mins.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}د` };
}

import { logEvent } from "@/lib/logger";

import * as CapCore from '@capacitor/core';
const { Capacitor } = CapCore;

export interface NoorAlarmPlugin {
  scheduleNativeAlarm(opts: { id: number; triggerAtMillis: number; title: string; body: string; soundType?: string }): Promise<{ scheduled: boolean }>;
  cancelNativeAlarm(opts: { id: number }): Promise<void>;
  triggerTestAlarmNow(opts?: { title?: string; body?: string; soundType?: string; adhanId?: string; durationSeconds?: number; volumePercent?: number }): Promise<void>;
  updatePrayerWidget?(opts: { nextPrayerText: string; subtitleText: string }): Promise<void>;
  requestAndGetLocation?(): Promise<{ granted: boolean; hasCoords: boolean; lat?: number; lng?: number }>;
}
export const NoorAlarm: NoorAlarmPlugin = typeof CapCore.registerPlugin === 'function'
  ? CapCore.registerPlugin<NoorAlarmPlugin>('NoorAlarm')
  : {
      scheduleNativeAlarm: async () => ({ scheduled: true }),
      cancelNativeAlarm: async () => {},
      triggerTestAlarmNow: async () => {},
    };
import { LocalNotifications } from '@capacitor/local-notifications';
import {
  LocalReminder,
  loadReminders,
  saveReminder,
  deleteReminderFromStorage,
  generateReminderId,
} from '@/lib/reminderStorage';

export { loadReminders } from '@/lib/reminderStorage';
import { getDhikr } from '@/lib/dhikrLibrary';

export type PermissionState = 'granted' | 'denied' | 'prompt' | 'unknown';

export type ScheduleResult = {
  success: boolean;
  error?: string;
  scheduledIds?: number[];
};

const ROLLING_HORIZON_DAYS = 14;

const DAY_NAMES_FA = ['شنبه', 'یکشنبه', 'دوشنبه', 'سه‌شنبه', 'چهارشنبه', 'پنجشنبه', 'جمعه'];

function ourDayToJsDay(ourDay: number): number {
  return (ourDay + 6) % 7;
}

function jsDayToOurDay(jsDay: number): number {
  return (jsDay + 1) % 7;
}

export function getDayLabel(ourDay: number): string {
  return DAY_NAMES_FA[ourDay] ?? '';
}

export { jsDayToOurDay, ourDayToJsDay };

export function getNextOccurrence(
  time: string,
  days: number[],
  fromNow: Date = new Date(),
  horizonDays: number = ROLLING_HORIZON_DAYS,
): Date | null {
  const [h, m] = time.split(':').map(Number);
  if (isNaN(h) || isNaN(m)) return null;

  for (let i = 0; i <= horizonDays; i++) {
    const candidate = new Date(fromNow.getFullYear(), fromNow.getMonth(), fromNow.getDate() + i, h, m, 0, 0);
    const jsDay = candidate.getDay();
    const ourDay = jsDayToOurDay(jsDay);
    const dayMatches = days.length === 0 || days.includes(ourDay);
    if (dayMatches && candidate.getTime() > fromNow.getTime()) {
      return candidate;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function getUpcomingOccurrences(
  time: string,
  days: number[],
  fromNow: Date = new Date(),
  horizonDays: number = ROLLING_HORIZON_DAYS,
): Date[] {
  const result: Date[] = [];
  const [h, m] = time.split(':').map(Number);
  if (isNaN(h) || isNaN(m)) return result;

  for (let i = 0; i <= horizonDays; i++) {
    const candidate = new Date(fromNow.getFullYear(), fromNow.getMonth(), fromNow.getDate() + i, h, m, 0, 0);
    const jsDay = candidate.getDay();
    const ourDay = jsDayToOurDay(jsDay);
    const dayMatches = days.length === 0 || days.includes(ourDay);
    if (dayMatches && candidate.getTime() > fromNow.getTime()) {
      result.push(candidate);
    }
  }
  return result;
}

export function parseDateTime(dateStr: string, time: string): Date | null {
  const [h, m] = time.split(':').map(Number);
  if (isNaN(h) || isNaN(m)) return null;
  const [y, mo, d] = dateStr.split('-').map(Number);
  if (isNaN(y) || isNaN(mo) || isNaN(d)) return null;
  return new Date(y, mo - 1, d, h, m, 0, 0);
}

export function isOneTimePast(date: string | null, time: string, now: Date = new Date()): boolean {
  if (!date) return true;
  const dt = parseDateTime(date, time);
  if (!dt) return true;
  return dt.getTime() <= now.getTime();
}

function reminderOccurrenceId(reminderId: string, occurrenceIndex: number): number {
  let hash = 0;
  const str = `${reminderId}:${occurrenceIndex}`;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + str.charCodeAt(i);
    hash = hash & 0x7fffffff;
  }
  return (hash % 900000) + 100000;
}

export function getReminderNotificationIds(reminderId: string, count: number = ROLLING_HORIZON_DAYS + 2): number[] {
  const ids: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    ids.push(reminderOccurrenceId(reminderId, i));
  }
  return ids;
}

export async function checkPermission(): Promise<PermissionState> {
  if (!Capacitor.isNativePlatform() && typeof window !== "undefined" && "Notification" in window) {
    const webPerm = Notification.permission === "default" ? "prompt" : (Notification.permission as PermissionState);
    return webPerm;
  }
  try {
    const { display } = await LocalNotifications.checkPermissions();
    if (display === 'granted') return 'granted';
    if (display === 'denied') return 'denied';
    if (display === 'prompt') return 'prompt';
    return 'unknown';
  } catch {
    return 'unknown';
  }
}

export async function requestPermission(): Promise<PermissionState> {
  logEvent("info", "ReminderService", "فراخوانی requestPermission");
  if (!Capacitor.isNativePlatform() && typeof window !== "undefined" && "Notification" in window) {
    const res = await Notification.requestPermission();
    return res === "default" ? "prompt" : (res as PermissionState);
  }
  try {
    const { display } = await LocalNotifications.requestPermissions();
    if (display === 'granted') return 'granted';
    if (display === 'denied') return 'denied';
    return 'prompt';
  } catch {
    return 'unknown';
  }
}

async function ensureChannel(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await LocalNotifications.createChannel({
      id: 'noor-reminders',
      name: 'یادآوری‌های نور',
      description: 'یادآوری‌های ذکر و نماز',
      importance: 4,
      visibility: 1,
      sound: undefined,
    });
  } catch {
    // channel may already exist or not supported on web
  }
}

export async function cancelReminder(reminderId: string): Promise<ScheduleResult> {
  const ids = getReminderNotificationIds(reminderId);
  try {
    await LocalNotifications.cancel({ notifications: ids.map((id) => ({ id })) });
    if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android') {
      for (const id of ids) { try { await NoorAlarm.cancelNativeAlarm({ id }); } catch {} }
    }
    return { success: true, scheduledIds: ids };
  } catch (err) {
    return { success: false, error: `خطا در لغو: ${err}` };
  }
}

export async function scheduleReminder(reminder: LocalReminder, notifEnabled: boolean = true): Promise<ScheduleResult> {
  if (!reminder.enabled) {
    await cancelReminder(reminder.id);
    return { success: true, scheduledIds: [] };
  }

  if (!notifEnabled) {
    await cancelReminder(reminder.id);
    return { success: true, scheduledIds: [] };
  }

  const perm = await checkPermission();
  if (perm !== 'granted') {
    return { success: false, error: 'اجازه اعلان داده نشده است' };
  }

  // Exact-alarm access is an Android-only setting. Calling this API on web/iOS
  // can fail even though those platforms do not require Android's exact-alarm grant.
  if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android') {
    try {
      const { exact_alarm } = await LocalNotifications.checkExactNotificationSetting();
      if (exact_alarm !== 'granted') {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: 'اجازه آلارم‌ها و یادآوری‌ها داده نشده است؛ دسترسی آلارم دقیق اندروید را بررسی کن.',
        };
      }
    } catch {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'وضعیت اجازه آلارم دقیق قابل بررسی نیست؛ تنظیمات اعلان اندروید را بررسی کن.',
      };
    }
  }

  await ensureChannel();

  const dhikr = reminder.dhikrId ? getDhikr(reminder.dhikrId) : null;
  const body = reminder.body || (dhikr ? dhikr.translation : 'یادآوری ذکر');

  await cancelReminder(reminder.id);

  if (reminder.type === 'once') {
    if (isOneTimePast(reminder.date, reminder.time)) {
      return { success: false, error: 'تاریخ و زمان یادآوری یکباره در گذشته است' };
    }
    const at = parseDateTime(reminder.date ?? '', reminder.time);
    if (!at) {
      return { success: false, error: 'تاریخ یا زمان نامعتبر است' };
    }
    const notifId = reminderOccurrenceId(reminder.id, 0);
    try {
      await LocalNotifications.schedule({
        notifications: [{
          id: notifId,
          title: reminder.title,
          body,
          channelId: 'noor-reminders',
          schedule: { at, allowWhileIdle: true },
          smallIcon: 'ic_stat_icon',
        }],
      });
      if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android') {
        try { await NoorAlarm.scheduleNativeAlarm({ id: notifId, triggerAtMillis: at.getTime(), title: reminder.title, body, soundType: reminder.id.startsWith('prayer_auto_') ? 'adhan' : 'alarm' }); } catch {}
      }
      return { success: true, scheduledIds: [notifId] };
    } catch (err) {
      return { success: false, error: `خطا در زمان‌بندی: ${err}` };
    }
  }

  const occurrences = getUpcomingOccurrences(reminder.time, reminder.days);
  if (occurrences.length === 0) {
    return { success: false, error: 'هیچ زمان مناسبی برای زمان‌بندی یافت نشد' };
  }

  const scheduledIds: number[] = [];
  const failed: string[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < occurrences.length; i++) {
    const notifId = reminderOccurrenceId(reminder.id, i);
    try {
      await LocalNotifications.schedule({
        notifications: [{
          id: notifId,
          title: reminder.title,
          body,
          channelId: 'noor-reminders',
          schedule: { at: occurrences[i], allowWhileIdle: true },
          smallIcon: 'ic_stat_icon',
        }],
      });
      if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android' && i < 3) {
        try { await NoorAlarm.scheduleNativeAlarm({ id: notifId, triggerAtMillis: occurrences[i].getTime(), title: reminder.title, body, soundType: reminder.id.startsWith('prayer_auto_') ? 'adhan' : 'alarm' }); } catch {}
      }
      scheduledIds.push(notifId);
    } catch (err) {
      failed.push(`${occurrences[i].toISOString()}: ${err}`);
    }
  }

  if (scheduledIds.length === 0) {
    return { success: false, error: `خطا در زمان‌بندی همه موارد: ${failed.join('; ')}` };
  }
  return { success: true, scheduledIds };
}

export async function createReminder(params: {
  title: string;
  time: string;
  days: number[];
  dhikrId: string | null;
  body?: string;
  type: 'recurring' | 'once';
  date?: string | null;
  notifEnabled?: boolean;
}): Promise<{ reminder: LocalReminder; schedule: ScheduleResult }> {
  const id = generateReminderId();
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  if (params.type === 'once') {
    if (isOneTimePast(params.date ?? null, params.time)) {
      return {
        reminder: {
          id, title: params.title, body: params.body ?? '', time: params.time,
          days: [], enabled: false, dhikrId: params.dhikrId,
          type: 'once', date: params.date ?? null,
          createdAt: now, updatedAt: now,
        },
        schedule: { success: false, error: 'تاریخ و زمان در گذشته است' },
      };
    }
  }

  const reminder: LocalReminder = {
    id,
    title: params.title,
    body: params.body ?? '',
    time: params.time,
    days: params.type === 'once' ? [] : params.days,
    enabled: true,
    dhikrId: params.dhikrId,
    type: params.type,
    date: params.type === 'once' ? (params.date ?? null) : null,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  };

  saveReminder(reminder);
  const notifEnabled = params.notifEnabled ?? true;
  const schedule = await scheduleReminder(reminder, notifEnabled);

  if (!schedule.success) {
    reminder.enabled = false;
    reminder.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    saveReminder(reminder);
  }

  return { reminder, schedule };
}

export async function updateReminder(
  id: string,
  updates: Partial<Pick<LocalReminder, 'title' | 'time' | 'days' | 'dhikrId' | 'body' | 'enabled' | 'type' | 'date'>>,
  notifEnabled: boolean = true,
): Promise<{ reminder: LocalReminder | null; schedule: ScheduleResult }> {
  const all = loadReminders();
  const existing = all.find((r) => r.id === id);
  if (!existing) return { reminder: null, schedule: { success: false, error: 'یادآوری یافت نشد' } };

  const updated: LocalReminder = {
    ...existing,
    ...updates,
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
  saveReminder(updated);

  await cancelReminder(id);
  const schedule = await scheduleReminder(updated, notifEnabled);

  if (!schedule.success && updates.enabled !== false) {
    updated.enabled = false;
    updated.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    saveReminder(updated);
  }

  return { reminder: updated, schedule };
}

export async function toggleReminder(id: string, notifEnabled: boolean = true): Promise<{ reminder: LocalReminder | null; schedule: ScheduleResult }> {
  const all = loadReminders();
  const existing = all.find((r) => r.id === id);
  if (!existing) return { reminder: null, schedule: { success: false, error: 'یادآوری یافت نشد' } };

  if (existing.enabled) {
    await cancelReminder(id);
    return updateReminder(id, { enabled: false }, notifEnabled);
  } else {
    return updateReminder(id, { enabled: true }, notifEnabled);
  }
}

export async function deleteReminder(id: string): Promise<ScheduleResult> {
  const schedule = await cancelReminder(id);
  deleteReminderFromStorage(id);
  return schedule;
}

export async function rescheduleAll(notifEnabled: boolean = true): Promise<{ total: number; succeeded: number; failed: number }> {
  const all = loadReminders();
  let succeeded = 0;
  let failed = 0;

  for (const reminder of all) {
    if (!reminder.enabled) continue;
    const result = await scheduleReminder(reminder, notifEnabled);
    if (result.success) succeeded++;
    else failed++;
  }

  return { total: all.filter((reminder) => reminder.enabled).length, succeeded, failed };
}

export async function getPendingNotifications(): Promise<number[]> {
  try {
    const { notifications } = await LocalNotifications.getPending();
    return notifications.map((n) => n.id);
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export async function cancelAllScheduledNotifications(): Promise<void> {
  const all = loadReminders();
  for (const r of all) {
    await cancelReminder(r.id);
  }
}

export async function rescheduleAllReminders(
  notifEnabled: boolean = true
): Promise<{ total: number; succeeded: number; failed: number }> {
  return rescheduleAll(notifEnabled);
}

export type { LocalReminder };
export { DAY_NAMES_FA, ROLLING_HORIZON_DAYS };


========================================
FILE: src/lib/reminderStorage.ts
========================================
export type ReminderType = 'recurring' | 'once';

export type LocalReminder = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  time: string;
  days: number[];
  enabled: boolean;
  dhikrId: string | null;
  type: ReminderType;
  date: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  durationDays?: number | null;
  intervalDays?: number | null;
  categoryTag?: 'prayer' | 'quran' | 'charity' | 'parents' | 'chilla' | 'general';
};

type ReminderStore = {
  version: number;
  reminders: Record<string, LocalReminder>;
};

const STORAGE_KEY = 'noor_reminders_store';
const COMPLETION_LOG_KEY = 'noor_reminders_completion_log_v1';
const SCHEMA_VERSION = 2;

function defaultStore(): ReminderStore {
  return { version: SCHEMA_VERSION, reminders: {} };
}

function getLocalDateKey(date: Date = new Date()): string {
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function migrate(raw: unknown): ReminderStore {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') return defaultStore();
  const obj = raw as Partial<ReminderStore> & { reminders?: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>> };

  if (!obj.version || obj.version < 1) return defaultStore();
  if (!obj.reminders || typeof obj.reminders !== 'object') return defaultStore();

  const reminders: Record<string, LocalReminder> = {};
  for (const [id, r] of Object.entries(obj.reminders)) {
    if (!r || typeof r.title !== 'string' || typeof r.time !== 'string' ||
        !Array.isArray(r.days) || typeof r.enabled !== 'boolean') {
      continue;
    }
    const type = (r.type === 'once' || r.type === 'recurring')
      ? (r.type as ReminderType)
      : 'recurring';
    const date = typeof r.date === 'string' ? r.date : null;
    reminders[id] = {
      id,
      title: r.title,
      body: typeof r.body === 'string' ? r.body : '',
      time: r.time,
      days: r.days as number[],
      enabled: r.enabled,
      dhikrId: typeof r.dhikrId === 'string' ? r.dhikrId : null,
      type,
      date,
      createdAt: typeof r.createdAt === 'string' ? r.createdAt : new Date().toISOString(),
      updatedAt: typeof r.updatedAt === 'string' ? r.updatedAt : new Date().toISOString(),
      durationDays: typeof r.durationDays === 'number' ? r.durationDays : null,
      intervalDays: typeof r.intervalDays === 'number' ? r.intervalDays : null,
      categoryTag: typeof r.categoryTag === 'string' ? (r.categoryTag as LocalReminder['categoryTag']) : 'general',
    };
  }
  return { version: SCHEMA_VERSION, reminders };
}

export function loadReminders(): LocalReminder[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const store = migrate(JSON.parse(raw));
    return Object.values(store.reminders).sort((a, b) => a.time.localeCompare(b.time));
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export function loadReminder(id: string): LocalReminder | null {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const store = migrate(JSON.parse(raw));
    return store.reminders[id] ?? null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function saveReminders(reminders: Record<string, LocalReminder>): void {
  try {
    const store: ReminderStore = { version: SCHEMA_VERSION, reminders };
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(store));
  } catch {
    // storage full or unavailable
  }
}

export function saveReminder(reminder: LocalReminder): void {
  const all = loadReminders();
  const map: Record<string, LocalReminder> = {};
  for (const r of all) map[r.id] = r;
  map[reminder.id] = reminder;
  saveReminders(map);
}

export function deleteReminderFromStorage(id: string): void {
  const all = loadReminders();
  const map: Record<string, LocalReminder> = {};
  for (const r of all) if (r.id !== id) map[r.id] = r;
  saveReminders(map);
}

export function clearAllReminders(): void {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export function generateReminderId(): string {
  return `r_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;
}

// سیستم ثبت و گزارش Planned vs. Actual (تیک زدن انجام کارهای برنامه‌ریزی‌شده)
type CompletionLogMap = Record<string, string[]>; // dateKey -> array of reminderIds completed that day

function loadCompletionLog(): CompletionLogMap {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(COMPLETION_LOG_KEY);
    if (!raw) return {};
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' ? (parsed as CompletionLogMap) : {};
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

function saveCompletionLog(log: CompletionLogMap): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(COMPLETION_LOG_KEY, JSON.stringify(log));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export function isReminderCompletedOnDate(reminderId: string, date: Date = new Date()): boolean {
  const key = getLocalDateKey(date);
  const log = loadCompletionLog();
  return Boolean(log[key]?.includes(reminderId));
}

export function toggleReminderCompletionOnDate(reminderId: string, date: Date = new Date()): boolean {
  const key = getLocalDateKey(date);
  const log = loadCompletionLog();
  const list = new Set(log[key] ?? []);
  let nextState = false;
  if (list.has(reminderId)) {
    list.delete(reminderId);
    nextState = false;
  } else {
    list.add(reminderId);
    nextState = true;
  }
  log[key] = Array.from(list);
  saveCompletionLog(log);
  return nextState;
}

export function getReminderTotalCompletedDays(reminderId: string): number {
  const log = loadCompletionLog();
  let count = 0;
  for (const ids of Object.values(log)) {
    if (Array.isArray(ids) && ids.includes(reminderId)) count++;
  }
  return count;
}

export function getPlannedVsActualStats(days: number = 30): {
  todayPlanned: number;
  todayCompleted: number;
  todayPercent: number;
  periodCompletedTotal: number;
} {
  const all = loadReminders().filter((r) => r.enabled);
  const today = new Date();
  const ourDay = (today.getDay() + 1) % 7;

  const todayReminders = all.filter((r) => {
    if (r.type === 'once') {
      return r.date === getLocalDateKey(today);
    }
    return r.days.length === 0 || r.days.includes(ourDay);
  });

  const todayPlanned = todayReminders.length;
  const todayCompleted = todayReminders.filter((r) => isReminderCompletedOnDate(r.id, today)).length;
  const todayPercent = todayPlanned > 0 ? Math.round((todayCompleted / todayPlanned) * 100) : 0;

  const log = loadCompletionLog();
  const cutoff = new Date();
  cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - days);
  const cutoffKey = getLocalDateKey(cutoff);

  let periodCompletedTotal = 0;
  for (const [dateKey, ids] of Object.entries(log)) {
    if (dateKey >= cutoffKey && Array.isArray(ids)) {
      periodCompletedTotal += ids.length;
    }
  }

  return {
    todayPlanned,
    todayCompleted,
    todayPercent,
    periodCompletedTotal,
  };
}

export { STORAGE_KEY, SCHEMA_VERSION };

import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

const configuredSupabaseUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL?.trim();
const configuredSupabaseAnonKey = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY?.trim();

function isValidSupabaseUrl(value: string | undefined): value is string {
  if (!value) return false;

  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    return (url.protocol === 'https:' || url.protocol === 'http:') &&
      Boolean(url.hostname) &&
      !url.username &&
      !url.password;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

const validSupabaseUrl = isValidSupabaseUrl(configuredSupabaseUrl);
export const isSupabaseConfigured = validSupabaseUrl && Boolean(configuredSupabaseAnonKey);

// Keep guest mode renderable even if build-time Supabase secrets are missing or
// malformed. Never pass an invalid environment value to createClient: it throws
// during module initialization and leaves the Android WebView on the splash screen.
export const supabase = createClient(
  validSupabaseUrl ? configuredSupabaseUrl : 'https://configuration-missing.supabase.co',
  isSupabaseConfigured ? configuredSupabaseAnonKey : 'configuration-missing-anon-key',
  {
    auth: {
      persistSession: true,
      autoRefreshToken: true,
      detectSessionInUrl: true,
    },
  },
);

  display_name: string;
  city_id: string;
  calculation_method: string;
  notif_enabled: boolean;
  theme_id: string;
  adhan_id: string;
};


========================================
FILE: src/lib/tasbihStorage.ts
========================================
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';

export type TasbihSession = {
  dhikrId: string;
  count: number;
  target: number;
  completed: number;
};

export type TasbihRecord = {
  id: string;
  dhikrId: string;
  count: number;
  completed: number;
  date: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

const GUEST_STORE_KEY = 'noor_tasbih_store';
const GUEST_SESSION_KEY = 'noor_tasbih_session';

type GuestStore = {
  records: TasbihRecord[];
};

function loadGuestStore(): GuestStore {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(GUEST_STORE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return { records: [] };
    return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    return { records: [] };
  }
}

function saveGuestStore(store: GuestStore): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(GUEST_STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(store));
  } catch {
    // storage unavailable
  }
}

function getLocalDateString(date: Date = new Date()): string {
  const d = date;
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

const DEFAULT_SESSION: TasbihSession = { dhikrId: '', count: 0, target: 33, completed: 0 };

function nonNegativeInteger(value: unknown, fallback: number): number {
  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0 ? value : fallback;
}

export function getSession(): TasbihSession {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(GUEST_SESSION_KEY);
    if (!raw) return { ...DEFAULT_SESSION };
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object') return { ...DEFAULT_SESSION };
    const s = parsed as Partial<TasbihSession>;
    const target = typeof s.target === 'number' && Number.isSafeInteger(s.target) && s.target > 0 && s.target <= 100000
      ? s.target
      : DEFAULT_SESSION.target;
    const count = Math.min(nonNegativeInteger(s.count, 0), target - 1);
    return {
      dhikrId: typeof s.dhikrId === 'string' ? s.dhikrId : '',
      count,
      target,
      completed: nonNegativeInteger(s.completed, 0),
    };
  } catch {
    return { ...DEFAULT_SESSION };
  }
}

export function saveSession(session: TasbihSession): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(GUEST_SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify(session));
  } catch {
    // storage unavailable
  }
}

export function clearSession(): void {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(GUEST_SESSION_KEY);
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export function startSession(dhikrId: string, target: number): void {
  const safeTarget = Number.isSafeInteger(target) && target > 0 && target <= 100000 ? target : DEFAULT_SESSION.target;
  saveSession({ dhikrId: typeof dhikrId === 'string' ? dhikrId : '', count: 0, target: safeTarget, completed: 0 });
}

export function incrementCount(userId?: string | null): { count: number; completed: number; reachedTarget: boolean } {
  const session = getSession();
  if (!session.dhikrId) {
    return { count: session.count, completed: session.completed, reachedTarget: false };
  }
  let newCount = session.count + 1;
  let newCompleted = session.completed;
  let reachedTarget = false;

  if (newCount >= session.target) {
    newCount = 0;
    newCompleted += 1;
    reachedTarget = true;
    saveRecord(session.dhikrId, session.target, 1, userId);
  }

  saveSession({ ...session, count: newCount, completed: newCompleted });
  return { count: newCount, completed: newCompleted, reachedTarget };
}

export function resetSession(): void {
  const session = getSession();
  saveSession({ ...session, count: 0 });
}

export function saveRecord(dhikrId: string, count: number, completed: number, userId?: string | null): void {
  const date = getLocalDateString();
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  if (userId) {
    supabase.from('tasbih_counts')
      .insert({ dhikr_id: dhikrId, count, completed, date, user_id: userId })
      .then(({ error }) => {
        if (error) {
          saveGuestRecord(dhikrId, count, completed, date, now);
        }
      });
  } else {
    saveGuestRecord(dhikrId, count, completed, date, now);
  }
}

function saveGuestRecord(dhikrId: string, count: number, completed: number, date: string, createdAt: string): void {
  const store = loadGuestStore();
  store.records.push({
    id: `r_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
    dhikrId,
    count,
    completed,
    date,
    createdAt,
  });
  if (store.records.length > 1000) store.records = store.records.slice(-1000);
  saveGuestStore(store);
}

export function getTodayTotal(userId?: string | null): number {
  const today = getLocalDateString();
  if (userId) {
    return 0;
  }
  const store = loadGuestStore();
  return store.records
    .filter((r) => r.date === today)
    .reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.count * r.completed, 0);
}

export function getTodayRounds(userId?: string | null): number {
  const today = getLocalDateString();
  if (userId) {
    return 0;
  }
  const store = loadGuestStore();
  return store.records
    .filter((r) => r.date === today)
    .reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.completed, 0);
}

export async function fetchTodayStats(userId: string): Promise<{ total: number; rounds: number }> {
  const today = getLocalDateString();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('tasbih_counts')
    .select('count, completed')
    .eq('user_id', userId)
    .eq('date', today);

  if (error || !data) return { total: 0, rounds: 0 };
  const total = data.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.count * r.completed, 0);
  const rounds = data.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.completed, 0);
  return { total, rounds };
}


export type PeriodRange = 7 | 30 | 90 | 365;

export function getSpiritualDashboardStats(days: PeriodRange = 30): {
  totalCount: number;
  totalRounds: number;
  activeDays: number;
  streakDays: number;
  topDhikrId: string | null;
  levelTitle: string;
} {
  const store = loadGuestStore();
  const cutoff = new Date();
  cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - days);
  const cutoffStr = getLocalDateString(cutoff);

  const filtered = store.records.filter((r) => r.date >= cutoffStr);
  const totalCount = filtered.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.count * r.completed, 0);
  const totalRounds = filtered.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.completed, 0);
  const uniqueDates = new Set(filtered.map((r) => r.date));

  const byDhikr: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const r of filtered) {
    byDhikr[r.dhikrId] = (byDhikr[r.dhikrId] ?? 0) + r.count * r.completed;
  }
  let topDhikrId: string | null = null;
  let maxVal = 0;
  for (const [id, val] of Object.entries(byDhikr)) {
    if (val > maxVal) {
      maxVal = val;
      topDhikrId = id;
    }
  }

  let streakDays = 0;
  const cursor = new Date();
  for (let i = 0; i < 365; i++) {
    const dStr = getLocalDateString(cursor);
    if (store.records.some((r) => r.date === dStr)) {
      streakDays++;
      cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - 1);
    } else if (i === 0) {
      cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - 1);
    } else {
      break;
    }
  }

  const allTimeCount = store.records.reduce((s, r) => s + r.count * r.completed, 0);
  let levelTitle = "رهجوی آغازگر 🌱";
  if (allTimeCount >= 10000) levelTitle = "سفره‌دار نور و ذکر 👑";
  else if (allTimeCount >= 3000) levelTitle = "ذاکر مداوم 💎";
  else if (allTimeCount >= 500) levelTitle = "همراه روشنایی ✨";

  return {
    totalCount,
    totalRounds,
    activeDays: uniqueDates.size,
    streakDays,
    topDhikrId,
    levelTitle,
  };
}

========================================
FILE: src/hooks/useAuth.tsx
========================================
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { supabase, isSupabaseConfigured, type Profile } from '@/lib/supabase';

type AuthContextType = {
  user: { id: string; email: string | null } | null;
  profile: Profile | null;
  loading: boolean;
  signIn: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<{ error: string | null }>;
  signUp: (email: string, password: string, displayName: string) => Promise<{ error: string | null }>;
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
  updateProfile: (updates: Partial<Profile>) => Promise<void>;
};

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const SUPABASE_CONFIG_ERROR = 'ورود و ثبت‌نام فعلاً تنظیم نشده است؛ تنظیمات Supabase برای این نسخه باید تکمیل شود.';

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<{ id: string; email: string | null } | null>(null);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<Profile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const fetchProfile = useCallback(async (uid: string) => {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', uid)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) {
      return;
    }

    if (!data) {
      const { error: insertError } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .insert({ id: uid });
      if (insertError) return;

      const { data: newData } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('*')
        .eq('id', uid)
        .maybeSingle();
      if (newData) setProfile(newData as Profile);
    } else {
      setProfile(data as Profile);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isSupabaseConfigured) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    let mounted = true;

    supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data: { session } }) => {
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (session?.user) {
        setUser({ id: session.user.id, email: session.user.email ?? null });
        void fetchProfile(session.user.id);
      }
      setLoading(false);
    }).catch(() => {
      if (mounted) setLoading(false);
    });

    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
      (async () => {
        if (session?.user) {
          setUser({ id: session.user.id, email: session.user.email ?? null });
          await fetchProfile(session.user.id);
        } else {
          setUser(null);
          setProfile(null);
        }
        setLoading(false);
      })();
    });

    return () => {
      mounted = false;
      subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, [fetchProfile]);

  const signIn = useCallback(async (email: string, password: string) => {
    if (!isSupabaseConfigured) return { error: SUPABASE_CONFIG_ERROR };
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
    if (error) return { error: error.message };
    return { error: null };
  }, []);

  const signUp = useCallback(async (email: string, password: string, displayName: string) => {
    if (!isSupabaseConfigured) return { error: SUPABASE_CONFIG_ERROR };
    const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({ email, password });
    if (error) return { error: error.message };
    if (data.user) {
      await supabase.from('profiles').upsert({
        id: data.user.id,
        display_name: displayName,
      });
    }
    return { error: null };
  }, []);

  const signOut = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured) await supabase.auth.signOut();
    setUser(null);
    setProfile(null);
  }, []);

  const updateProfile = useCallback(async (updates: Partial<Profile>) => {
    if (!user || !isSupabaseConfigured) return;
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .update({ ...updates, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('id', user.id);
    if (error) return;
    setProfile((prev) => prev ? { ...prev, ...updates } : prev);
  }, [user]);

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, profile, loading, signIn, signUp, signOut, updateProfile }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AuthProvider');
  return ctx;
}


========================================
FILE: src/hooks/useTasbih.ts
========================================
import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  getSession,
  saveSession,
  startSession,
  incrementCount,
  resetSession,
  clearSession,
} from '@/lib/tasbihStorage';

export function useTasbih(userId?: string | null) {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(getSession());

  const start = useCallback((dhikrId: string, target: number) => {
    startSession(dhikrId, target);
    setSession(getSession());
  }, []);

  const increment = useCallback(() => {
    const result = incrementCount(userId);
    setSession(getSession());
    return result;
  }, [userId]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    resetSession();
    setSession(getSession());
  }, []);

  const clear = useCallback(() => {
    clearSession();
    setSession(getSession());
  }, []);

  const save = useCallback((s: typeof session) => {
    saveSession(s);
    setSession(getSession());
  }, [session]);

  return { session, start, increment, reset, clear, save };
}


========================================
FILE: src/hooks/useTheme.tsx
========================================
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { themes, getThemeById, type Theme } from '@/lib/themes';
import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';

const GUEST_THEME_KEY = 'noor_theme_id';

type ThemeContextType = {
  theme: Theme;
  themeId: string;
  setThemeId: (id: string) => void;
};

const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { profile } = useAuth();
  const [themeId, setThemeIdState] = useState<string>(() => {
    try {
      return localStorage.getItem(GUEST_THEME_KEY) ?? 'emerald';
    } catch {
      return 'emerald';
    }
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (profile?.theme_id) {
      setThemeIdState(profile.theme_id);
    }
  }, [profile?.theme_id]);

  const setThemeId = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setThemeIdState(id);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(GUEST_THEME_KEY, id);
    } catch {
      // localStorage unavailable
    }
  }, []);

  const theme = getThemeById(themeId);

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    root.style.setProperty('--primary', theme.primary);
    root.style.setProperty('--primary-glow', theme.primaryGlow);
    root.style.setProperty('--accent', theme.accent);
    root.style.setProperty('--bg', theme.bg);
    root.style.setProperty('--surface', theme.surface);
    root.style.setProperty('--surface-alt', theme.surfaceAlt);
    root.style.setProperty('--text', theme.text);
    root.style.setProperty('--text-muted', theme.textMuted);
    root.style.setProperty('--border', theme.border);
    document.body.style.background = theme.gradient;
    document.body.style.color = theme.text;
  }, [theme]);

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ theme, themeId, setThemeId }}>
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useTheme() {
  const ctx = useContext(ThemeContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useTheme must be used within ThemeProvider');
  return ctx;
}

export { themes };

FILE: android/app/src/main/java/com/noor/app/AdhanAlarmActivity.java
