package com.noor.app;

import android.app.KeyguardManager;
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.IntentFilter;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.graphics.Typeface;
import android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.Gravity;
import android.view.WindowManager;
import android.widget.Button;
import android.widget.LinearLayout;
import android.widget.TextView;
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;

public class AdhanAlarmActivity extends AppCompatActivity {
    public static final String ACTION_CLOSE_UI = "com.noor.app.ACTION_CLOSE_ALARM_UI";

    private final BroadcastReceiver closeReceiver = new BroadcastReceiver() {
        @Override
        public void onReceive(Context context, Intent intent) {
            finishAndRemoveTask();
        }
    };

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        turnScreenOnAndUnlock();

        String titleText = getIntent() != null && getIntent().getStringExtra("title") != null
                ? getIntent().getStringExtra("title")
                : "اذان و یادآوری نور";
        String bodyText = getIntent() != null && getIntent().getStringExtra("body") != null
                ? getIntent().getStringExtra("body")
                : "حَیَّ عَلَی الصَّلَاة — وقت آرامش و نیایش";

        LinearLayout root = new LinearLayout(this);
        root.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
        root.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
        root.setBackgroundColor(Color.parseColor("#062E22"));
        root.setPadding(72, 72, 72, 72);

        TextView badge = new TextView(this);
        badge.setText("🌿 اپلیکیشن نور");
        badge.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#34D399"));
        badge.setTextSize(18f);
        badge.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);

        TextView title = new TextView(this);
        title.setText(titleText);
        title.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#FEF3C7"));
        title.setTextSize(32f);
        title.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);
        title.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams titleParams = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
        );
        titleParams.setMargins(0, 36, 0, 24);
        title.setLayoutParams(titleParams);

        TextView body = new TextView(this);
        body.setText(bodyText);
        body.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#D1FAE5"));
        body.setTextSize(18f);
        body.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams bodyParams = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
        );
        bodyParams.setMargins(0, 0, 0, 96);
        body.setLayoutParams(bodyParams);

        Button stopBtn = new Button(this);
        stopBtn.setText("توقف صدا و بستن صفحه");
        stopBtn.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#062E22"));
        stopBtn.setTextSize(18f);
        stopBtn.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD);

        GradientDrawable btnBg = new GradientDrawable();
        btnBg.setColor(Color.parseColor("#FBBF24"));
        btnBg.setCornerRadius(48f);
        stopBtn.setBackground(btnBg);
        stopBtn.setPadding(64, 36, 64, 36);

        stopBtn.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            Intent stopIntent = new Intent(this, AdhanForegroundService.class);
            stopIntent.setAction(AdhanForegroundService.ACTION_STOP);
            startService(stopIntent);
            finishAndRemoveTask();
        });

        root.addView(badge);
        root.addView(title);
        root.addView(body);
        root.addView(stopBtn);
        setContentView(root);

        IntentFilter filter = new IntentFilter(ACTION_CLOSE_UI);
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            registerReceiver(closeReceiver, filter, Context.RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED);
        } else {
            registerReceiver(closeReceiver, filter);
        }
    }

    private void turnScreenOnAndUnlock() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O_MR1) {
            setShowWhenLocked(true);
            setTurnScreenOn(true);
            KeyguardManager km = (KeyguardManager) getSystemService(Context.KEYGUARD_SERVICE);
            if (km != null) {
                km.requestDismissKeyguard(this, null);
            }
        }
        getWindow().addFlags(
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_SHOW_WHEN_LOCKED |
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_DISMISS_KEYGUARD |
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_TURN_SCREEN_ON |
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON
        );
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDestroy() {
        try {
            unregisterReceiver(closeReceiver);
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        super.onDestroy();
    }
}


========================================
FILE: android/app/src/main/java/com/noor/app/AdhanAlarmReceiver.java
========================================
package com.noor.app;

import android.content.BroadcastReceiver;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.os.Build;

public class AdhanAlarmReceiver extends BroadcastReceiver {
    public static final String ACTION_TRIGGER = "com.noor.app.ACTION_TRIGGER_ADHAN_ALARM";
    public static final String ACTION_STOP = "com.noor.app.ACTION_STOP_ADHAN_ALARM";

    @Override
    public void onReceive(Context context, Intent intent) {
        if (intent == null || intent.getAction() == null) return;

        if (ACTION_STOP.equals(intent.getAction())) {
            Intent stopService = new Intent(context, AdhanForegroundService.class);
            stopService.setAction(ACTION_STOP);
            context.stopService(stopService);
            context.sendBroadcast(new Intent("com.noor.app.CLOSE_ALARM_UI"));
            return;
        }

        if (ACTION_TRIGGER.equals(intent.getAction())) {
            Intent serviceIntent = new Intent(context, AdhanForegroundService.class);
            serviceIntent.putExtra("title", intent.getStringExtra("title"));
            serviceIntent.putExtra("body", intent.getStringExtra("body"));
            serviceIntent.putExtra("soundType", intent.getStringExtra("soundType"));
            serviceIntent.putExtra("adhanId", intent.getStringExtra("adhanId"));
            serviceIntent.putExtra("durationSeconds", intent.getIntExtra("durationSeconds", 0));
            serviceIntent.putExtra("volumePercent", intent.getIntExtra("volumePercent", 90));

            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                context.startForegroundService(serviceIntent);
            } else {
                context.startService(serviceIntent);
            }
        }
    }
}


========================================
FILE: android/app/src/main/java/com/noor/app/AdhanForegroundService.java
========================================
package com.noor.app;

import android.app.Notification;
import android.app.NotificationChannel;
import android.app.NotificationManager;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.app.Service;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.media.AudioAttributes;
import android.media.MediaPlayer;
import android.media.RingtoneManager;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.IBinder;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.os.PowerManager;
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat;
import java.io.File;

public class AdhanForegroundService extends Service {
    public static final String ACTION_STOP = "com.noor.app.STOP_ADHAN";
    public static final String CHANNEL_ID = "noor_adhan_fullscreen_channel_v3";
    private MediaPlayer mediaPlayer;
    private PowerManager.WakeLock wakeLock;
    private final Handler stopHandler = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
    private final Runnable stopRunnable = this::stopSelf;

    @Override
    public void onCreate() {
        super.onCreate();
        createHighPriorityChannel();
        PowerManager pm = (PowerManager) getSystemService(POWER_SERVICE);
        if (pm != null) {
            wakeLock = pm.newWakeLock(
                    PowerManager.PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK | PowerManager.ACQUIRE_CAUSES_WAKEUP,
                    "NoorApp::AdhanWakeLock"
            );
            wakeLock.acquire(4 * 60 * 1000L);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public int onStartCommand(Intent intent, int flags, int startId) {
        if (intent != null && AdhanAlarmReceiver.ACTION_STOP.equals(intent.getAction())) {
            stopSelf();
            return START_NOT_STICKY;
        }

        String title = intent != null && intent.getStringExtra("title") != null
                ? intent.getStringExtra("title") : "وقت اذان و نیایش";
        String body = intent != null && intent.getStringExtra("body") != null
                ? intent.getStringExtra("body") : "حَیَّ عَلَی الصَّلَاة — وقت نماز فرا رسیده است";
        String soundType = intent != null && intent.getStringExtra("soundType") != null
                ? intent.getStringExtra("soundType") : "adhan";
        String adhanId = intent != null && intent.getStringExtra("adhanId") != null
                ? intent.getStringExtra("adhanId") : "mahur_saeed";
        int durationSeconds = intent != null ? intent.getIntExtra("durationSeconds", 0) : 0;
        int volumePercent = intent != null ? intent.getIntExtra("volumePercent", 90) : 90;

        Intent fullScreenIntent = new Intent(this, AdhanAlarmActivity.class);
        fullScreenIntent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK | Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP | Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP);
        fullScreenIntent.putExtra("title", title);
        fullScreenIntent.putExtra("body", body);

        PendingIntent fullScreenPendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
                this, 2001, fullScreenIntent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        );

        Intent stopIntent = new Intent(this, AdhanAlarmReceiver.class);
        stopIntent.setAction(AdhanAlarmReceiver.ACTION_STOP);
        PendingIntent stopPendingIntent = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(
                this, 2002, stopIntent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        );

        Notification notification = new NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
                .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.noor_launcher)
                .setContentTitle(title)
                .setContentText(body)
                .setStyle(new NotificationCompat.BigTextStyle().bigText(body))
                .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_MAX)
                .setCategory(NotificationCompat.CATEGORY_ALARM)
                .setVisibility(NotificationCompat.VISIBILITY_PUBLIC)
                .setOngoing(true)
                .setAutoCancel(false)
                .setFullScreenIntent(fullScreenPendingIntent, true)
                .addAction(android.R.drawable.ic_media_pause, "توقف صدا", stopPendingIntent)
                .build();

        startForeground(3001, notification);

        if (!"silent".equals(soundType)) {
            startAudioPlayback(soundType, adhanId, volumePercent, durationSeconds);
        }

        try {
            startActivity(fullScreenIntent);
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        return START_NOT_STICKY;
    }

    private void startAudioPlayback(String soundType, String adhanId, int volumePercent, int durationSeconds) {
        stopAudioPlayback();
        try {
            mediaPlayer = new MediaPlayer();
            AudioAttributes attrs = new AudioAttributes.Builder()
                    .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_ALARM)
                    .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_MUSIC)
                    .build();
            mediaPlayer.setAudioAttributes(attrs);

            float vol = Math.max(0.05f, Math.min(1.0f, volumePercent / 100.0f));

            if ("adhan".equals(soundType) || "short_10s".equals(soundType)) {
                File downloadedFile = new File(new File(getFilesDir(), "adhans"), adhanId + ".mp3");
                if (downloadedFile.exists() && downloadedFile.length() > 4096) {
                    mediaPlayer.setDataSource(downloadedFile.getAbsolutePath());
                } else {
                    Uri rawUri = Uri.parse("android.resource://" + getPackageName() + "/" + R.raw.adhan_mahur);
                    mediaPlayer.setDataSource(this, rawUri);
                }
                mediaPlayer.setLooping(false);
                mediaPlayer.setOnCompletionListener(mp -> stopSelf());
            } else {
                Uri alarmUri = RingtoneManager.getDefaultUri(RingtoneManager.TYPE_ALARM);
                if (alarmUri == null) {
                    alarmUri = RingtoneManager.getDefaultUri(RingtoneManager.TYPE_NOTIFICATION);
                }
                mediaPlayer.setDataSource(this, alarmUri);
                mediaPlayer.setLooping(durationSeconds <= 0);
            }

            mediaPlayer.prepare();
            mediaPlayer.setVolume(vol, vol);
            mediaPlayer.start();

            int limitSec = "short_10s".equals(soundType) ? 10 : durationSeconds;
            if (limitSec > 0) {
                stopHandler.removeCallbacks(stopRunnable);
                stopHandler.postDelayed(stopRunnable, limitSec * 1000L);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void stopAudioPlayback() {
        stopHandler.removeCallbacks(stopRunnable);
        if (mediaPlayer != null) {
            try {
                if (mediaPlayer.isPlaying()) mediaPlayer.stop();
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            try {
                mediaPlayer.release();
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            mediaPlayer = null;
        }
    }

    private void createHighPriorityChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            NotificationChannel channel = new NotificationChannel(
                    CHANNEL_ID,
                    "آلارم تمام‌صفحه و اذان نور",
                    NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_HIGH
            );
            channel.setDescription("پخش اذان با صدای مؤذن انتخابی و بیدار کردن صفحه قفل");
            channel.setSound(null, null);
            channel.enableVibration(true);
            channel.setLockscreenVisibility(Notification.VISIBILITY_PUBLIC);
            NotificationManager nm = getSystemService(NotificationManager.class);
            if (nm != null) nm.createNotificationChannel(channel);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroy() {
        stopAudioPlayback();
        if (wakeLock != null && wakeLock.isHeld()) {
            wakeLock.release();
        }
        super.onDestroy();
    }

    @Override
    public IBinder onBind(Intent intent) {
        return null;
    }
}

package com.noor.app;

import android.graphics.Color;
import android.os.Bundle;
import com.getcapacitor.BridgeActivity;

public class MainActivity extends BridgeActivity {
    @Override
    public void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        registerPlugin(NoorAlarmPlugin.class);
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        try {
            getWindow().getDecorView().setBackgroundColor(Color.parseColor("#062E22"));
            if (getBridge() != null && getBridge().getWebView() != null) {
                getBridge().getWebView().setBackgroundColor(Color.parseColor("#062E22"));
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }
}


========================================
FILE: android/app/src/main/java/com/noor/app/NoorAlarmPlugin.java
========================================
package com.noor.app;

import android.Manifest;
import android.app.AlarmManager;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetManager;
import android.content.ComponentName;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.location.Location;
import android.location.LocationManager;
import android.media.AudioAttributes;
import android.media.MediaPlayer;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.widget.RemoteViews;
import com.getcapacitor.JSArray;
import com.getcapacitor.JSObject;
import com.getcapacitor.PermissionState;
import com.getcapacitor.Plugin;
import com.getcapacitor.PluginCall;
import com.getcapacitor.PluginMethod;
import com.getcapacitor.annotation.CapacitorPlugin;
import com.getcapacitor.annotation.Permission;
import com.getcapacitor.annotation.PermissionCallback;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
import java.net.URL;

@CapacitorPlugin(
    name = "NoorAlarm",
    permissions = {
        @Permission(
            alias = "location",
            strings = {
                Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION,
                Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION
            }
        )
    }
)
public class NoorAlarmPlugin extends Plugin {
    private MediaPlayer previewPlayer;

    @PluginMethod
    public void requestAndGetLocation(PluginCall call) {
        if (getPermissionState("location") != PermissionState.GRANTED) {
            requestPermissionForAlias("location", call, "locationPermsCallback");
        } else {
            resolveBestLocation(call);
        }
    }

    @PermissionCallback
    private void locationPermsCallback(PluginCall call) {
        if (getPermissionState("location") == PermissionState.GRANTED) {
            resolveBestLocation(call);
        } else {
            call.reject("LOCATION_PERMISSION_DENIED");
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("MissingPermission")
    private void resolveBestLocation(PluginCall call) {
        try {
            LocationManager lm = (LocationManager) getContext().getSystemService(Context.LOCATION_SERVICE);
            Location best = null;
            if (lm != null) {
                for (String provider : lm.getProviders(true)) {
                    Location l = lm.getLastKnownLocation(provider);
                    if (l != null && (best == null || l.getAccuracy() < best.getAccuracy())) {
                        best = l;
                    }
                }
            }
            JSObject res = new JSObject();
            res.put("granted", true);
            if (best != null) {
                res.put("lat", best.getLatitude());
                res.put("lng", best.getLongitude());
                res.put("hasCoords", true);
            } else {
                res.put("hasCoords", false);
            }
            call.resolve(res);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            call.reject("LOCATION_ERROR: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @PluginMethod
    public void downloadAdhanAudio(PluginCall call) {
        String adhanId = call.getString("adhanId", "");
        String audioUrl = call.getString("audioUrl", "");
        if (adhanId == null || adhanId.isEmpty() || audioUrl == null || audioUrl.isEmpty()) {
            call.reject("INVALID_PARAMS");
            return;
        }

        new Thread(() -> {
            HttpURLConnection conn = null;
            try {
                File dir = new File(getContext().getFilesDir(), "adhans");
                if (!dir.exists()) dir.mkdirs();
                File targetFile = new File(dir, adhanId + ".mp3");

                URL url = new URL(audioUrl);
                conn = (HttpURLConnection) url.openConnection();
                conn.setInstanceFollowRedirects(true);
                conn.setConnectTimeout(15000);
                conn.setReadTimeout(30000);
                conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "NoorSpiritualApp/2.0 (Android)");
                conn.connect();

                int code = conn.getResponseCode();
                if (code >= 300 && code < 400) {
                    String loc = conn.getHeaderField("Location");
                    if (loc != null) {
                        conn.disconnect();
                        conn = (HttpURLConnection) new URL(loc).openConnection();
                        conn.setConnectTimeout(15000);
                        conn.setReadTimeout(30000);
                        conn.connect();
                    }
                }

                try (InputStream in = conn.getInputStream();
                     FileOutputStream out = new FileOutputStream(targetFile)) {
                    byte[] buf = new byte[8192];
                    int len;
                    long total = 0;
                    while ((len = in.read(buf)) != -1) {
                        out.write(buf, 0, len);
                        total += len;
                    }
                    out.flush();
                    if (total < 4096) {
                        targetFile.delete();
                        call.reject("DOWNLOAD_TOO_SMALL");
                        return;
                    }
                }

                JSObject res = new JSObject();
                res.put("downloaded", true);
                res.put("adhanId", adhanId);
                res.put("path", targetFile.getAbsolutePath());
                call.resolve(res);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                call.reject("DOWNLOAD_FAILED: " + e.getMessage());
            } finally {
                if (conn != null) conn.disconnect();
            }
        }).start();
    }

    @PluginMethod
    public void listDownloadedAdhans(PluginCall call) {
        JSArray arr = new JSArray();
        arr.put("mahur_saeed");
        File dir = new File(getContext().getFilesDir(), "adhans");
        if (dir.exists() && dir.isDirectory()) {
            File[] files = dir.listFiles();
            if (files != null) {
                for (File f : files) {
                    if (f.isFile() && f.length() > 4096 && f.getName().endsWith(".mp3")) {
                        arr.put(f.getName().replace(".mp3", ""));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        JSObject res = new JSObject();
        res.put("ids", arr);
        call.resolve(res);
    }

    @PluginMethod
    public void previewAdhanAudio(PluginCall call) {
        String adhanId = call.getString("adhanId", "mahur_saeed");
        String audioUrl = call.getString("audioUrl", "");
        int durationSeconds = call.getInt("durationSeconds", 15);
        int volumePercent = call.getInt("volumePercent", 90);

        stopPreviewInternal();
        try {
            previewPlayer = new MediaPlayer();
            AudioAttributes attrs = new AudioAttributes.Builder()
                    .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_MEDIA)
                    .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_MUSIC)
                    .build();
            previewPlayer.setAudioAttributes(attrs);

            File localFile = new File(new File(getContext().getFilesDir(), "adhans"), adhanId + ".mp3");
            if (localFile.exists() && localFile.length() > 4096) {
                previewPlayer.setDataSource(localFile.getAbsolutePath());
            } else if ("mahur_saeed".equals(adhanId) || audioUrl == null || audioUrl.isEmpty()) {
                Uri rawUri = Uri.parse("android.resource://" + getContext().getPackageName() + "/" + R.raw.adhan_mahur);
                previewPlayer.setDataSource(getContext(), rawUri);
            } else {
                previewPlayer.setDataSource(audioUrl);
            }

            float vol = Math.max(0.05f, Math.min(1.0f, volumePercent / 100.0f));
            previewPlayer.setOnPreparedListener(mp -> {
                mp.setVolume(vol, vol);
                mp.start();
            });
            previewPlayer.prepareAsync();

            if (durationSeconds > 0) {
                new android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(
                        this::stopPreviewInternal,
                        durationSeconds * 1000L
                );
            }
            call.resolve();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            call.reject("PREVIEW_ERROR: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @PluginMethod
    public void stopPreviewAudio(PluginCall call) {
        stopPreviewInternal();
        call.resolve();
    }

    private void stopPreviewInternal() {
        if (previewPlayer != null) {
            try {
                if (previewPlayer.isPlaying()) previewPlayer.stop();
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            try {
                previewPlayer.release();
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            previewPlayer = null;
        }
    }

    @PluginMethod
    public void updatePrayerWidget(PluginCall call) {
        String nextPrayerText = call.getString("nextPrayerText", "اوقات شرعی نور");
        String subtitleText = call.getString("subtitleText", "برای مشاهده اوقات شرعی لمس کنید");
        try {
            Context context = getContext();
            AppWidgetManager gm = AppWidgetManager.getInstance(context);
            ComponentName cn = new ComponentName(context, NoorPrayerWidgetProvider.class);
            int[] ids = gm.getAppWidgetIds(cn);
            if (ids != null && ids.length > 0) {
                RemoteViews views = new RemoteViews(context.getPackageName(), R.layout.noor_prayer_widget);
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_next_prayer, nextPrayerText);
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_subtitle, subtitleText);
                gm.updateAppWidget(ids, views);
            }
            call.resolve();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            call.resolve();
        }
    }

    @PluginMethod
    public void scheduleNativeAlarm(PluginCall call) {
        Integer id = call.getInt("id", 1001);
        Long triggerAtMillis = call.getLong("triggerAtMillis", 0L);
        String title = call.getString("title", "یادآوری نور");
        String body = call.getString("body", "وقت ذکر و آرامش");
        String soundType = call.getString("soundType", "adhan");
        String adhanId = call.getString("adhanId", "mahur_saeed");
        Integer durationSeconds = call.getInt("durationSeconds", 0);
        Integer volumePercent = call.getInt("volumePercent", 90);

        if (triggerAtMillis == null || triggerAtMillis <= System.currentTimeMillis()) {
            call.reject("Invalid triggerAtMillis");
            return;
        }

        Context context = getContext();
        AlarmManager am = (AlarmManager) context.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);
        if (am == null) {
            call.reject("AlarmManager unavailable");
            return;
        }

        Intent intent = new Intent(context, AdhanAlarmReceiver.class);
        intent.setAction(AdhanAlarmReceiver.ACTION_TRIGGER);
        intent.putExtra("title", title);
        intent.putExtra("body", body);
        intent.putExtra("soundType", soundType);
        intent.putExtra("adhanId", adhanId);
        intent.putExtra("durationSeconds", durationSeconds != null ? durationSeconds : 0);
        intent.putExtra("volumePercent", volumePercent != null ? volumePercent : 90);

        PendingIntent operation = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(
                context,
                id,
                intent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        );

        Intent showIntent = new Intent(context, MainActivity.class);
        PendingIntent showPending = PendingIntent.getActivity(
                context,
                id,
                showIntent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        );

        AlarmManager.AlarmClockInfo clockInfo = new AlarmManager.AlarmClockInfo(triggerAtMillis, showPending);
        am.setAlarmClock(clockInfo, operation);

        JSObject res = new JSObject();
        res.put("scheduled", true);
        call.resolve(res);
    }

    @PluginMethod
    public void cancelNativeAlarm(PluginCall call) {
        Integer id = call.getInt("id", 1001);
        Context context = getContext();
        AlarmManager am = (AlarmManager) context.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);

        Intent intent = new Intent(context, AdhanAlarmReceiver.class);
        intent.setAction(AdhanAlarmReceiver.ACTION_TRIGGER);

        PendingIntent operation = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(
                context,
                id,
                intent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        );

        if (am != null) am.cancel(operation);
        operation.cancel();
        call.resolve();
    }

    @PluginMethod
    public void triggerTestAlarmNow(PluginCall call) {
        Context context = getContext();
        String title = call.getString("title", "اذان به افق شهر شما");
        String body = call.getString("body", "حَیَّ عَلَی الصَّلَاة — تست پخش اذان و باز شدن صفحه قفل");
        String soundType = call.getString("soundType", "adhan");
        String adhanId = call.getString("adhanId", "mahur_saeed");
        Integer durationSeconds = call.getInt("durationSeconds", 0);
        Integer volumePercent = call.getInt("volumePercent", 90);

        Intent intent = new Intent(context, AdhanAlarmReceiver.class);
        intent.setAction(AdhanAlarmReceiver.ACTION_TRIGGER);
        intent.putExtra("title", title);
        intent.putExtra("body", body);
        intent.putExtra("soundType", soundType);
        intent.putExtra("adhanId", adhanId);
        intent.putExtra("durationSeconds", durationSeconds != null ? durationSeconds : 0);
        intent.putExtra("volumePercent", volumePercent != null ? volumePercent : 90);
        context.sendBroadcast(intent);
        call.resolve();
    }
}

FILE: android/app/src/main/java/com/noor/app/NoorPrayerWidgetProvider.java

package com.noor.app;

import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetManager;
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetProvider;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.widget.RemoteViews;

public class NoorPrayerWidgetProvider extends AppWidgetProvider {
    @Override
    public void onUpdate(Context context, AppWidgetManager appWidgetManager, int[] appWidgetIds) {
        for (int appWidgetId : appWidgetIds) {
            Intent intent = new Intent(context, MainActivity.class);
            PendingIntent pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
                    context, 0, intent, PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
            );
            RemoteViews views = new RemoteViews(context.getPackageName(), R.layout.noor_prayer_widget);
            views.setOnClickPendingIntent(R.id.widget_root, pendingIntent);
            appWidgetManager.updateAppWidget(appWidgetId, views);
        }
    }
}

========================================
FILE: src/components/AdhanStudioAndTravelModal.tsx
========================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { Sliders, Plane, Building2, Volume2, X, Check, Play, Square } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme } from '@/hooks/useTheme';
import {
  loadAdhanStudio,
  saveAdhanStudio,
  loadMosqueAndTravel,
  saveMosqueAndTravel,
  type SoundProfileId,
  type PrayerAlarmMode,
} from '@/lib/deenIntelligenceStore';
import { adhans, playAdhanPreview, stopAdhanPreview } from '@/lib/adhans';
import { CITIES, setGuestCityId } from '@/lib/cities';
import { toFaDigits } from '@/lib/jalali';

interface Props {
  initialSection?: 'studio' | 'travel' | 'mosque';
  onClose: () => void;
  onChanged?: () => void;
}

const PRAYER_LABELS: Array<{ key: 'fajr' | 'dhuhr' | 'asr' | 'maghrib' | 'isha'; nameFa: string }> = [
  { key: 'fajr', nameFa: 'نماز صبح' },
  { key: 'dhuhr', nameFa: 'نماز ظهر' },
  { key: 'asr', nameFa: 'نماز عصر' },
  { key: 'maghrib', nameFa: 'نماز مغرب' },
  { key: 'isha', nameFa: 'نماز عشاء' },
];

export function AdhanStudioAndTravelModal({ initialSection = 'studio', onClose, onChanged }: Props) {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'studio' | 'travel' | 'mosque'>(initialSection);
  const [studio, setStudio] = useState(() => loadAdhanStudio());
  const [mt, setMt] = useState(() => loadMosqueAndTravel());
  const [playingPrayer, setPlayingPrayer] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [savedNotice, setSavedNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const activeProf = studio.profiles[studio.activeProfile];

  const handleSelectProfile = (profId: SoundProfileId) => {
    const next = { ...studio, activeProfile: profId };
    setStudio(next);
    saveAdhanStudio(next);
    if (profId === 'travel' && !mt.travelModeEnabled) {
      const nextMt = { ...mt, travelModeEnabled: true };
      setMt(nextMt);
      saveMosqueAndTravel(nextMt);
    }
    onChanged?.();
  };

  const updatePrayerPatch = (
    pKey: 'fajr' | 'dhuhr' | 'asr' | 'maghrib' | 'isha',
    patch: Partial<(typeof activeProf.prayers)['fajr']>
  ) => {
    const next = {
      ...studio,
      profiles: {
        ...studio.profiles,
        [studio.activeProfile]: {
          ...activeProf,
          prayers: {
            ...activeProf.prayers,
            [pKey]: {
              ...activeProf.prayers[pKey],
              ...patch,
            },
          },
        },
      },
    };
    setStudio(next);
    saveAdhanStudio(next);
    onChanged?.();
  };

  const handlePreviewPrayerAudio = async (pKey: 'fajr' | 'dhuhr' | 'asr' | 'maghrib' | 'isha') => {
    if (playingPrayer === pKey) {
      stopAdhanPreview();
      setPlayingPrayer(null);
      return;
    }
    const cfg = activeProf.prayers[pKey];
    const targetAdhan = adhans.find((a) => a.id === cfg.adhanId) ?? adhans[0];
    setPlayingPrayer(pKey);
    const dur = cfg.mode === 'short_10s' ? 10 : 18;
    try {
      await playAdhanPreview(targetAdhan, dur, cfg.volumePercent);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  };

  const handleSaveMosqueTravel = (nextMt: typeof mt) => {
    setMt(nextMt);
    saveMosqueAndTravel(nextMt);
    if (nextMt.travelModeEnabled) {
      setGuestCityId(nextMt.destinationCityId);
    }
    setSavedNotice('✓ تنظیمات هوشمند ذخیره و روی صفحهٔ اول اعمال شد.');
    setTimeout(() => setSavedNotice(null), 2500);
    onChanged?.();
  };

  const node = (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[999] overflow-y-auto flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-fade-in"
      style={{ background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.82)', backdropFilter: 'blur(6px)' }}
      dir="rtl"
    >
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-5 border shadow-2xl my-auto max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto"
        style={{ background: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border }}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-3 border-b" style={{ borderColor: theme.border }}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Sliders size={19} color={theme.primary} />
            <h2 className="text-base font-black text-app">استودیوی شخصی اذان، سفر و مسجد</h2>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              stopAdhanPreview();
              onClose();
            }}
            className="text-muted p-1"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {savedNotice && (
          <div
            className="mb-3 rounded-2xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-center"
            style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
          >
            {savedNotice}
          </div>
        )}

        <div
          className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 p-1 rounded-2xl mb-4"
          style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 10%, transparent)` }}
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTab('studio')}
            className="py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1"
            style={{
              background: tab === 'studio' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              color: tab === 'studio' ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
            }}
          >
            <Volume2 size={13} /> استودیو اذان
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTab('travel')}
            className="py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1"
            style={{
              background: tab === 'travel' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              color: tab === 'travel' ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
            }}
          >
            <Plane size={13} /> حالت سفر
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTab('mosque')}
            className="py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1"
            style={{
              background: tab === 'mosque' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              color: tab === 'mosque' ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
            }}
          >
            <Building2 size={13} /> برنامه مسجد
          </button>
        </div>

        {tab === 'studio' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-muted block mb-2">
                پروفایل موقعیت (خانه / محل کار / مسافرت):
              </label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                {(['home', 'work', 'travel'] as SoundProfileId[]).map((pid) => {
                  const p = studio.profiles[pid];
                  const active = studio.activeProfile === pid;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={pid}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleSelectProfile(pid)}
                      className="p-2.5 rounded-2xl border text-center transition-all"
                      style={{
                        borderColor: active ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                        background: active ? `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 16%, transparent)` : 'transparent',
                      }}
                    >
                      <span className="text-xs font-black block text-app">{p.badge}</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-primary font-bold mt-1 block">
                        {active ? 'فعال ✓' : 'انتخاب'}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-muted mt-2 leading-relaxed">{activeProf.descriptionFa}</p>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-app">تنظیم اختصاصی هر نماز در «{activeProf.nameFa}»:</p>
              {PRAYER_LABELS.map((pl) => {
                const cfg = activeProf.prayers[pl.key];
                const isPlaying = playingPrayer === pl.key;

                return (
                  <div key={pl.key} className="card-flat p-3.5 space-y-2.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="text-xs font-black text-app">{pl.nameFa}</span>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => void handlePreviewPrayerAudio(pl.key)}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1"
                        style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
                      >
                        {isPlaying ? <Square size={11} /> : <Play size={11} />}
                        <span>{isPlaying ? 'توقف پیش‌نمایش' : 'پیش‌نمایش صدا'}</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {/* انتخاب مؤذن اختصاصی برای همین نماز */}
                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[10px] text-muted block mb-1">مؤذن اختصاصی این نماز:</label>
                      <select
                        value={cfg.adhanId}
                        onChange={(e) => updatePrayerPatch(pl.key, { adhanId: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
                        style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
                      >
                        {adhans.map((a) => (
                          <option key={a.id} value={a.id}>
                            {a.reciter} — {a.nameFa}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    {/* حالت پخش: اذان کامل | ۱۰ ثانیه اول | اعلان کوتاه | بی‌صدا */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1">
                      {[
                        { id: 'full_adhan' as PrayerAlarmMode, label: '🕌 کامل' },
                        { id: 'short_10s' as PrayerAlarmMode, label: '⏱️ ۱۰ ثانیه' },
                        { id: 'short_beep' as PrayerAlarmMode, label: '🔔 کوتاه' },
                        { id: 'silent' as PrayerAlarmMode, label: '🔕 بی‌صدا' },
                      ].map((m) => (
                        <button
                          key={m.id}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => updatePrayerPatch(pl.key, { mode: m.id })}
                          className="py-1.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-bold border"
                          style={{
                            borderColor: cfg.mode === m.id ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                            background: cfg.mode === m.id ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
                            color: cfg.mode === m.id ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
                          }}
                        >
                          {m.label}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>

                    {/* تنظیم درصد بلندی صدا */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">بلندی صدا: {toFaDigits(cfg.volumePercent)}٪</span>
                      <input
                        type="range"
                        min={10}
                        max={100}
                        step={5}
                        value={cfg.volumePercent}
                        onChange={(e) => updatePrayerPatch(pl.key, { volumePercent: Number(e.target.value) })}
                        className="w-36 accent-emerald-400"
                      />
                    </div>

                    {/* نوتیفیکیشن دعای کوتاه قبل از اذان (۰، ۱۰، ۱۵ یا ۳۰ دقیقه قبل) */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 pt-1 border-t" style={{ borderColor: theme.border }}>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">اعلان دعای کوتاه قبل از اذان:</span>
                      <div className="flex gap-1">
                        {[
                          { min: 0, label: 'خاموش' },
                          { min: 10, label: '۱۰د قبل' },
                          { min: 15, label: '۱۵د قبل' },
                          { min: 30, label: '۳۰د قبل' },
                        ].map((opt) => (
                          <button
                            key={opt.min}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => updatePrayerPatch(pl.key, { preReminderMinutes: opt.min })}
                            className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold"
                            style={{
                              background:
                                cfg.preReminderMinutes === opt.min
                                  ? theme.accent
                                  : `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.textMuted} 15%, transparent)`,
                              color: cfg.preReminderMinutes === opt.min ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
                            }}
                          >
                            {opt.label}
                          </button>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {tab === 'travel' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="card-flat p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-app">فعال‌سازی حالت سفر هوشمند ✈️</p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-muted mt-0.5">
                  تنظیم خودکار اوقات شرعی مقصد و یادآوری احکام نماز شکسته (قصر)
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleSaveMosqueTravel({ ...mt, travelModeEnabled: !mt.travelModeEnabled })}
                className="w-11 h-6 rounded-full transition-all relative shrink-0"
                style={{ background: mt.travelModeEnabled ? theme.primary : `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.textMuted} 25%, transparent)` }}
              >
                <div className="absolute top-0.5 w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-white transition-all" style={{ right: mt.travelModeEnabled ? '2px' : '22px' }} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-muted block mb-1">شهر مبدا (وطن)</label>
                <select
                  value={mt.originCityId}
                  onChange={(e) => handleSaveMosqueTravel({ ...mt, originCityId: e.target.value })}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
                  style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
                >
                  {CITIES.map((c) => (
                    <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.nameFa}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-muted block mb-1">شهر مقصد سفر</label>
                <select
                  value={mt.destinationCityId}
                  onChange={(e) => handleSaveMosqueTravel({ ...mt, destinationCityId: e.target.value })}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
                  style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
                >
                  {CITIES.map((c) => (
                    <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.nameFa}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="card-flat p-3.5 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-app">نوع اقامه در مقصد (قصر / تمام)</p>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-muted mt-0.5">
                  در سفرهای زیر ۱۰ روز (بیش از مسافت شرعی)، نمازهای ۴ رکعتی شکسته (۲ رکعت) است.
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleSaveMosqueTravel({ ...mt, isQasrPrayer: !mt.isQasrPrayer })}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shrink-0"
                style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
              >
                {mt.isQasrPrayer ? 'شکسته (۲ رکعتی)' : 'تمام (قصد ۱۰ روز)'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {tab === 'mosque' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-muted block mb-1.5">نام مسجد محله یا محل کار شما</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={mt.selectedMosqueName}
                onChange={(e) => setMt({ ...mt, selectedMosqueName: e.target.value })}
                placeholder="مثلاً: مسجد جامع، مسجدالنبی یا حرم مطهر"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
                style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-app mb-2">
                فاصله زمانی اقامه جماعت مسجد پس از اذان افق شهر (دقیقه):
              </p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                {([
                  { key: 'fajr' as const, label: 'جماعت صبح' },
                  { key: 'dhuhr' as const, label: 'جماعت ظهر' },
                  { key: 'maghrib' as const, label: 'جماعت مغرب' },
                ]).map((item) => (
                  <div key={item.key} className="card-flat p-2.5 text-center">
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-muted block mb-1">{item.label}</span>
                    <select
                      value={mt.jamaatOffsetsMinutes[item.key]}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setMt({
                          ...mt,
                          jamaatOffsetsMinutes: {
                            ...mt.jamaatOffsetsMinutes,
                            [item.key]: Number(e.target.value),
                          },
                        })
                      }
                      className="w-full px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold text-primary border outline-none text-center"
                      style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
                    >
                      {[5, 10, 15, 20, 30].map((m) => (
                        <option key={m} value={m}>+{toFaDigits(m)} دقیقه</option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-muted block mb-1.5">برنامه یا اطلاعیه مسجد</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={mt.mosqueAnnouncement}
                onChange={(e) => setMt({ ...mt, mosqueAnnouncement: e.target.value })}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
                style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
              />
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleSaveMosqueTravel(mt)}
              className="w-full py-3 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold"
              style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
            >
              ذخیره برنامه مسجد
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            stopAdhanPreview();
            onClose();
          }}
          className="w-full mt-5 py-3 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 border"
          style={{ borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.text }}
        >
          <Check size={15} />
          <span>بستن</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return typeof document !== 'undefined' ? createPortal(node, document.body) : node;
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { Bell, Sparkles, Calendar, BookOpen, Check, ArrowLeft, X, Volume2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme } from '@/hooks/useTheme';

export const TUTORIAL_SEEN_KEY = 'noor_tutorial_seen_v1';

interface Props {
  onClose: () => void;
}

const SLIDES = [
  {
    icon: Bell,
    title: '۱. اذان خودکار و روشن شدن صفحه قفل',
    desc: 'در صفحهٔ اول، کنار ساعت هر نماز روی آیکون زنگوله (🔔) بزنید تا حتی وقتی گوشی قفل است، صفحه روشن شده و اذان پخش شود.',
  },
  {
    icon: Calendar,
    title: '۲. تقویم سه‌گانه و تبدیل تاریخ',
    desc: 'در بالای صفحهٔ اول روی تاریخ امروز بزنید تا تقویم شمسی، قمری و میلادی، اعمال مخصوص هر روز هفته و «مبدل تاریخ» برایتان باز شود.',
  },
  {
    icon: Sparkles,
    title: '۳. مدیریت برنامه امروز، چله، چک و تولد',
    desc: 'در بخش یادآوری‌ها می‌توانید چلهٔ ۴۰ روزه، فاتحهٔ والدین، سررسید چک/قسط و تولد عزیزان را ثبت کنید و هر روز با زدن تیک (✓) کارنامهٔ خود را کامل کنید.',
  },
  {
    icon: Volume2,
    title: '۴. دانلود اذان‌های ایرانی و انتخاب فونت',
    desc: 'در بخش تنظیمات می‌توانید اذان مؤذن‌زاده اردبیلی، آقاتی، صبحدل و ماهور را دانلود و انتخاب کنید و فونت دلخواه خود را تنظیم نمایید.',
  },
  {
    icon: BookOpen,
    title: '۵. ذکرشمار فوری متصل به گنجینه اذکار',
    desc: 'در بخش ذکرها، توصیهٔ علما و سوره‌ها را همراه با سند ببینید و مستقیم وارد حلقهٔ ذکرشمار بدون تاخیر شوید.',
  },
];

export function AppTutorialModal({ onClose }: Props) {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const current = SLIDES[step];
  const Icon = current.icon;

  const handleFinish = () => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(TUTORIAL_SEEN_KEY, 'true');
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
    onClose();
  };

  const node = (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[999] flex items-center justify-center p-5 animate-fade-in"
      style={{ background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.76)', backdropFilter: 'blur(5px)' }}
      dir="rtl"
    >
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl p-6 text-center relative border shadow-2xl"
        style={{ background: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border }}
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleFinish}
          aria-label="بستن راهنما"
          className="absolute top-4 left-4 text-muted"
        >
          <X size={20} />
        </button>

        <div
          className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl mx-auto mb-4 flex items-center justify-center"
          style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 16%, transparent)` }}
        >
          <Icon size={32} color={theme.primary} />
        </div>

        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-app mb-2">{current.title}</h2>
        <p className="text-muted text-sm leading-relaxed mb-6">{current.desc}</p>

        <div className="flex justify-center gap-1.5 mb-5">
          {SLIDES.map((_, idx) => (
            <span
              key={idx}
              className="h-2 rounded-full transition-all"
              style={{
                width: idx === step ? '24px' : '8px',
                background: idx === step ? theme.primary : theme.border,
              }}
            />
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {step < SLIDES.length - 1 ? (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleFinish}
                className="px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl text-xs font-medium text-muted"
              >
                رد کردن
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setStep((s) => s + 1)}
                className="flex-1 py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
              >
                <span>بعدی</span>
                <ArrowLeft size={16} />
              </button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleFinish}
              className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
            >
              <Check size={18} />
              <span>شروع استفاده از نور</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return typeof document !== 'undefined' ? createPortal(node, document.body) : node;
}


========================================
FILE: src/components/AuthScreen.tsx
========================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Moon, Mail, Lock, User, AlertCircle, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme } from '@/hooks/useTheme';
import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';

export function AuthScreen({ onBack }: { onBack?: () => void }) {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const { signIn, signUp } = useAuth();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'signin' | 'signup'>('signin');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      if (mode === 'signup') {
        if (!displayName.trim()) {
          setError('نام نمایشی را وارد کنید');
          return;
        }
        const { error: err } = await signUp(email, password, displayName.trim());
        if (err) setError(translateError(err));
      } else {
        const { error: err } = await signIn(email, password);
        if (err) setError(translateError(err));
      }
    } catch {
      setError('ارتباط برقرار نشد. اتصال اینترنت را بررسی کنید و دوباره تلاش کنید.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm">
        {onBack && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onBack}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted mb-5"
          >
            <ArrowRight size={18} /> بازگشت به برنامه
          </button>
        )}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center mb-8">
          <div
            className="w-20 h-20 rounded-3xl flex items-center justify-center mb-4"
            style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 15%, transparent)` }}
          >
            <Moon size={40} color={theme.primary} />
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-app">نور</h1>
          <p className="text-muted text-sm mt-1">همراه معنوی شما</p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-2 mb-5 p-1 rounded-2xl" style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 8%, transparent)` }}>
          <button
            onClick={() => { setMode('signin'); setError(null); }}
            className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all"
            style={{
              background: mode === 'signin' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'signin' ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
            }}
          >
            ورود
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => { setMode('signup'); setError(null); }}
            className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all"
            style={{
              background: mode === 'signup' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'signup' ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
            }}
          >
            ثبت‌نام
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3">
          {mode === 'signup' && (
            <div className="relative">
              <User size={18} color={theme.textMuted} className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="نام نمایشی"
                value={displayName}
                onChange={(e) => setDisplayName(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full pr-11 pl-4 py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-sm text-app bg-transparent border outline-none transition-colors"
                style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.surface} 50%, transparent)` }}
              />
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="relative">
            <Mail size={18} color={theme.textMuted} className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
            <input
              type="email"
              placeholder="ایمیل"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              required
              className="w-full pr-11 pl-4 py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-sm text-app bg-transparent border outline-none transition-colors"
              style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.surface} 50%, transparent)` }}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="relative">
            <Lock size={18} color={theme.textMuted} className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
            <input
              type="password"
              placeholder="رمز عبور"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              required
              minLength={6}
              className="w-full pr-11 pl-4 py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-sm text-app bg-transparent border outline-none transition-colors"
              style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.surface} 50%, transparent)` }}
            />
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-xl" style={{ background: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.1)' }}>
              <AlertCircle size={16} color="#fca5a5" className="flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <p className="text-xs" style={{ color: '#fca5a5' }}>{error}</p>
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
            style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
          >
            {loading ? '...' : mode === 'signin' ? 'ورود' : 'ثبت‌نام'}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function translateError(err: string): string {
  if (err.includes('Invalid login credentials')) return 'ایمیل یا رمز عبور اشتباه است';
  if (err.includes('User already registered')) return 'این ایمیل قبلاً ثبت شده است';
  if (err.includes('Password should be at least')) return 'رمز عبور باید حداقل ۶ کاراکتر باشد';
  if (err.includes('Unable to validate email')) return 'ایمیل نامعتبر است';
  return err;
}


========================================
FILE: src/components/BottomNav.tsx
========================================
import { Home, BookOpen, Bell, Settings, Heart } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme } from '@/hooks/useTheme';

export type Tab = 'home' | 'tasbih' | 'library' | 'reminders' | 'settings';

const tabs: { id: Tab; label: string; icon: typeof Home }[] = [
  { id: 'home', label: 'خانه', icon: Home },
  { id: 'tasbih', label: 'ذکرشمار', icon: Heart },
  { id: 'library', label: 'ذکرها', icon: BookOpen },
  { id: 'reminders', label: 'یادآوری', icon: Bell },
  { id: 'settings', label: 'تنظیمات', icon: Settings },
];

export function BottomNav({ active, onChange }: { active: Tab; onChange: (t: Tab) => void }) {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  return (
    <nav
      className="fixed bottom-0 z-50 grid grid-cols-5 gap-0 overflow-x-clip px-1 pt-2 pb-[calc(0.5rem+env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px))] backdrop-blur-lg"
      style={{
        left: 0,
        right: 0,
        width: "100%",
        maxWidth: "100vw",
        boxSizing: "border-box",
        background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.surface} 85%, transparent)`,
        borderTop: `1px solid ${theme.border}`,
      }}
    >
      {tabs.map((t) => {
        const Icon = t.icon;
        const isActive = active === t.id;
        return (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            onClick={() => onChange(t.id)}
            className="box-border flex w-full min-w-0 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 overflow-hidden px-0 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition-all"
            style={{
              background: isActive ? `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 15%, transparent)` : 'transparent',
            }}
          >
            <Icon size={22} color={isActive ? theme.primary : theme.textMuted} />
            <span
              className="max-w-full truncate text-[10px] font-medium leading-tight"
              style={{ color: isActive ? theme.primary : theme.textMuted }}
            >
              {t.label}
            </span>
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </nav>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/CalendarModal.tsx

import { useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { Calendar, X, ChevronRight, ChevronLeft, Sparkles, ArrowLeftRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme } from '@/hooks/useTheme';
import { toJalaliShort, getJalaliWeekday, toFaDigits, jalaliMonths, jalaliToGregorian, getJalaliParts } from '@/lib/jalali';
import { calculatePrayerTimes } from '@/lib/prayerTimes';

interface Props {
  cityLat: number;
  cityLng: number;
  cityTimezone: string;
  cityName: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const DAILY_WIRD: Record<number, { dayFa: string; imam: string; dhikr: string; count: number; deed: string }> = {
  6: { dayFa: 'شنبه', imam: 'متعلق به پیامبر اکرم (ص)', dhikr: 'يَا رَبَّ الْعَالَمِينَ', count: 100, deed: 'قرائت زیارت پیامبر (ص) و آغاز برنامه‌های هفتگی با صلوات' },
  0: { dayFa: 'یکشنبه', imam: 'متعلق به امیرالمؤمنین (ع) و حضرت زهرا (س)', dhikr: 'يَا ذَا الْجَلَالِ وَالْإِكْرَامِ', count: 100, deed: 'زیارت امیرالمؤمنین (ع) و حضرت زهرا (س) و صدقه برای سلامتی خانواده' },
  1: { dayFa: 'دوشنبه', imam: 'متعلق به امام حسن و امام حسین (ع)', dhikr: 'يَا قَاضِيَ الْحَاجَاتِ', count: 100, deed: 'تلاوت سوره واقعه و سلام به سیدالشهدا (ع)' },
  2: { dayFa: 'سه‌شنبه', imam: 'متعلق به امام سجاد، امام باقر و امام صادق (ع)', dhikr: 'يَا أَرْحَمَ الرَّاحِمِينَ', count: 100, deed: 'قرائت دعای توبه و استغفار در صحیفه سجادیه' },
  3: { dayFa: 'چهارشنبه', imam: 'متعلق به امام کاظم، امام رضا، امام جواد و امام هادی (ع)', dhikr: 'يَا حَيُّ يَا قَيُّومُ', count: 100, deed: 'زیارت مخصوص امام رضا (ع) و صلوات خاصه آن حضرت' },
  4: { dayFa: 'پنجشنبه', imam: 'متعلق به امام حسن عسکری (ع)', dhikr: 'لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ الْمَلِكُ الْحَقُّ الْمُبِينُ', count: 100, deed: 'فاتحه و خیرات برای والدین و اهل قبور و سلام شب جمعه در غروب' },
  5: { dayFa: 'جمعه', imam: 'متعلق به حضرت ولی‌عصر امام زمان (عج)', dhikr: 'اللَّهُمَّ صَلِّ عَلَى مُحَمَّدٍ وَآلِ مُحَمَّدٍ وَعَجِّلْ فَرَجَهُمْ', count: 100, deed: 'دعای ندبه، زیارت آل‌یاسین و دعای سمات در عصر جمعه' },
};

export function CalendarModal({ cityLat, cityLng, cityTimezone, cityName, onClose }: Props) {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'calendar' | 'converter'>('calendar');
  const [dayOffset, setDayOffset] = useState(0);

  const todayParts = getJalaliParts(new Date());
  const [convType, setConvType] = useState<'jalali_to_greg' | 'greg_to_jalali'>('jalali_to_greg');
  const [jYear, setJYear] = useState(todayParts.jy);
  const [jMonth, setJMonth] = useState(todayParts.jm);
  const [jDay, setJDay] = useState(todayParts.jd);
  const [gDateStr, setGDateStr] = useState(() => new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10));

  const selectedDate = new Date();
  selectedDate.setDate(selectedDate.getDate() + dayOffset);

  const pTimes = calculatePrayerTimes(selectedDate, cityLat, cityLng, cityTimezone, 'jafari');
  const gregorianStr = selectedDate.toLocaleDateString('en-CA');
  const weekdayIndex = selectedDate.getDay();
  const wird = DAILY_WIRD[weekdayIndex] ?? DAILY_WIRD[5];

  // محاسبه نتیجه مبدل تاریخ
  let convertedDate = new Date();
  if (convType === 'jalali_to_greg') {
    const [gy, gm, gd] = jalaliToGregorian(jYear, jMonth, jDay);
    convertedDate = new Date(gy, gm - 1, gd);
  } else {
    const [gy, gm, gd] = gDateStr.split('-').map(Number);
    if (!isNaN(gy) && !isNaN(gm) && !isNaN(gd)) {
      convertedDate = new Date(gy, gm - 1, gd);
    }
  }
  const convPrayer = calculatePrayerTimes(convertedDate, cityLat, cityLng, cityTimezone, 'jafari');
  const convGregStr = `${convertedDate.getFullYear()}/${String(convertedDate.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}/${String(convertedDate.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  const modalContent = (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[999] overflow-y-auto flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-fade-in"
      style={{ background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.78)', backdropFilter: 'blur(6px)' }}
      dir="rtl"
    >
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-5 border shadow-2xl my-auto max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto"
        style={{ background: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border }}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-3 border-b" style={{ borderColor: theme.border }}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Calendar size={20} color={theme.primary} />
            <h2 className="text-base font-black text-app">تقویم سه‌گانه و مبدل تاریخ</h2>
          </div>
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="text-muted p-1">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* تب‌بندی بین تقویم روزانه و مبدل تاریخ */}
        <div
          className="flex gap-2 p-1 rounded-2xl mb-4"
          style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 10%, transparent)` }}
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMode('calendar')}
            className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
            style={{
              background: mode === 'calendar' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'calendar' ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
            }}
          >
            <Calendar size={14} /> تقویم و اعمال روز
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMode('converter')}
            className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
            style={{
              background: mode === 'converter' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'converter' ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
            }}
          >
            <ArrowLeftRight size={14} /> تبدیل تاریخ
          </button>
        </div>

        {mode === 'calendar' ? (
          <>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-4">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDayOffset((d) => d - 1)}
                className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1 border"
                style={{ borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.text }}
              >
                <ChevronRight size={15} />
                <span>روز قبل</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDayOffset(0)}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
                style={{
                  background: dayOffset === 0 ? theme.primary : `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 14%, transparent)`,
                  color: dayOffset === 0 ? theme.bg : theme.primary,
                }}
              >
                امروز
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDayOffset((d) => d + 1)}
                className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1 border"
                style={{ borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.text }}
              >
                <span>روز بعد</span>
                <ChevronLeft size={15} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div
              className="rounded-2xl p-4 mb-4 text-center border"
              style={{
                background: `radial-gradient(circle at top, color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 18\%, transparent),${theme.bg})`,
                borderColor: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 35\%,${theme.border})`,
              }}
            >
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-primary mb-1">{getJalaliWeekday(selectedDate)}</p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-black text-app mb-2">{toJalaliShort(selectedDate)} خورشیدی</p>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted pt-2 border-t" style={{ borderColor: theme.border }}>
                <span>🌙 {pTimes.hijriDate} قمری</span>
                <span>·</span>
                <span dir="ltr">🌍 {gregorianStr}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="card-flat p-4 mb-4 space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Sparkles size={13} /> ذکر و نسبت روز {wird.dayFa}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-accent">{toFaDigits(wird.count)} مرتبه</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted">{wird.imam}</p>
              <p className="text-lg font-bold text-app text-center py-2">{wird.dhikr}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted leading-relaxed">✨ عمل توصیه‌شده: {wird.deed}</p>
            </div>

            <div className="mb-4">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-muted mb-2">اوقات شرعی این روز به افق {cityName}:</p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center text-xs">
                <div className="card-flat p-2">
                  <span className="text-muted block text-[10px]">اذان صبح</span>
                  <strong className="text-app" dir="ltr">{pTimes.fajr}</strong>
                </div>
                <div className="card-flat p-2">
                  <span className="text-muted block text-[10px]">اذان ظهر</span>
                  <strong className="text-app" dir="ltr">{pTimes.dhuhr}</strong>
                </div>
                <div className="card-flat p-2">
                  <span className="text-muted block text-[10px]">اذان مغرب</span>
                  <strong className="text-app" dir="ltr">{pTimes.maghrib}</strong>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4 mb-4">
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setConvType('jalali_to_greg')}
                className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border"
                style={{
                  borderColor: convType === 'jalali_to_greg' ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                  color: convType === 'jalali_to_greg' ? theme.primary : theme.textMuted,
                }}
              >
                شمسی به قمری و میلادی
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setConvType('greg_to_jalali')}
                className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border"
                style={{
                  borderColor: convType === 'greg_to_jalali' ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                  color: convType === 'greg_to_jalali' ? theme.primary : theme.textMuted,
                }}
              >
                میلادی به شمسی و قمری
              </button>
            </div>

            {convType === 'jalali_to_greg' ? (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                <div>
                  <label className="text-[11px] text-muted block mb-1">روز</label>
                  <select
                    value={jDay}
                    onChange={(e) => setJDay(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full px-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
                    style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
                  >
                    {Array.from({ length: 31 }, (_, i) => i + 1).map((d) => (
                      <option key={d} value={d}>{toFaDigits(d)}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="text-[11px] text-muted block mb-1">ماه شمسی</label>
                  <select
                    value={jMonth}
                    onChange={(e) => setJMonth(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full px-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
                    style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
                  >
                    {jalaliMonths.map((m, idx) => (
                      <option key={m} value={idx + 1}>{m}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="text-[11px] text-muted block mb-1">سال شمسی</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="1300"
                    max="1500"
                    value={jYear}
                    onChange={(e) => setJYear(Number(e.target.value) || 1405)}
                    className="w-full px-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs text-app border outline-none text-center"
                    style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
                    dir="ltr"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs text-muted block mb-1">انتخاب تاریخ میلادی</label>
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={gDateStr}
                  onChange={(e) => setGDateStr(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
                  style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
                  dir="ltr"
                />
              </div>
            )}

            <div
              className="rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-2 border text-center"
              style={{
                background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 10%, transparent)`,
                borderColor: theme.primary,
              }}
            >
              <p className="text-xs text-muted">نتیجهٔ تبدیل تاریخ ({getJalaliWeekday(convertedDate)}):</p>
              <p className="text-lg font-black text-primary">☀️ {toJalaliShort(convertedDate)} شمسی</p>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-app">🌙 {convPrayer.hijriDate} قمری</p>
              <p className="text-xs font-mono text-muted" dir="ltr">🌍 {convGregStr} (Gregorian)</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="w-full py-3 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold"
          style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
        >
          بستن
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return typeof document !== 'undefined' ? createPortal(modalContent, document.body) : modalContent;
}

const prayerIcons: Record<string, typeof Sun> = {
  fajr: Sunrise,
  sunrise: Sun,

  asr: CloudSun,
  maghrib: Sunset,
  isha: Moon,
