function getContextualSpiritualTip(now: Date, nextKey?: string): {
  badge: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  dhikrId: string;
} {
  const day = now.getDay(); // 4 = Thursday, 5 = Friday
  const hour = now.getHours();

  if (day === 4 && hour >= 16) {
    return {
      badge: 'ویژه شب جمعه',
      title: 'سلام به سیدالشهدا (ع) زیر آسمان و سوره واقعه',
      subtitle: 'صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْكَ يَا أَبَا عَبْدِ اللَّهِ — ۳ مرتبه با توجه قلبی',
      dhikrId: 'friday_night_husayn',
    };
  }
  if (nextKey === 'maghrib' || nextKey === 'isha' || hour >= 19) {
    return {
      badge: 'برنامه شبانه مجرب',
      title: 'تلاوت سوره واقعه و ذکر اللَّهُمَّ عَافِنَا وَاعْفُ عَنَّا',
      subtitle: 'توصیه امام صادق (ع) برای برکت رزق و توصیه آیت‌الله فاطمی‌نیا',
      dhikrId: 'fateminia_afina',
    };
  }
  if (hour >= 4 && hour < 11) {
    return {
      badge: 'برنامه آغاز روز',
      title: 'تلاوت سوره ذاریات و دعای اللَّهُمَّ سَلِّمْ وَ تَمِّمْ',
      subtitle: 'برای اصلاح معاش روزانه، حفظ ایمان و عاقبت‌به‌خیری',
      dhikrId: 'fateminia_sallim',
    };
  }
  return {
    badge: 'ذکر گره‌گشای روز',
    title: 'توسل به امام رئوف (ع) و تسبیحات حضرت زهرا (س)',
    subtitle: 'يَا رَءُوفُ يَا رَحِيمُ — کلید آرامش دل و گشایش کارها',
    dhikrId: 'imam_reza_hajat',
  };
}

export function HomeScreen({ onNavigate }: { onNavigate?: (t: Tab) => void }) {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const { profile, user, updateProfile } = useAuth();
  const [times, setTimes] = useState<PrayerTimes | null>(null);
  const [next, setNext] = useState<{ key: string; nameFa: string; time: string; remaining: string; progress: number } | null>(null);
  const [now, setNow] = useState(new Date());
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ total: 0, rounds: 0 });
  const [hasActiveSession, setHasActiveSession] = useState(false);
  const [enabledAlarms, setEnabledAlarms] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [statusToast, setStatusToast] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showQibla, setShowQibla] = useState(false);
  const [showCalendar, setShowCalendar] = useState(false);
  const [showAnalyticsModal, setShowAnalyticsModal] = useState(false);
  const [showStudioModal, setShowStudioModal] = useState(false);
  const [showQuranModal, setShowQuranModal] = useState(false);
  const [intelTick, setIntelTick] = useState(0);
  const [showTutorial, setShowTutorial] = useState(false);
  const [guestCityId, setGuestCityIdState] = useState(getGuestCityId());

  const cityId = profile?.city_id ?? guestCityId;
  const city = useMemo(() => getCityById(cityId), [cityId]);
  const method = profile?.calculation_method ?? 'jafari';
  const todayDate = getLocalDateString(now, city.timezone);

  useEffect(() => {
    const refreshNow = () => setNow(new Date());
    const interval = setInterval(refreshNow, 1000);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', refreshNow);
    window.addEventListener('focus', refreshNow);
    return () => {
      clearInterval(interval);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', refreshNow);
      window.removeEventListener('focus', refreshNow);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user?.id) {
      fetchTodayStats(user.id).then(setStats);
    } else {
      setStats({ total: getTodayTotal(), rounds: getTodayRounds() });
    }
    const session = getSession();
    setHasActiveSession(session.count > 0 || session.completed > 0);
  }, [todayDate, user?.id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = calculatePrayerTimes(now, city.lat, city.lng, city.timezone, method);
    t.location = city.nameFa;
    setTimes(t);

    const all = loadReminders();
    const map: Record<string, boolean> = {};
    const prayerMap: Record<string, { title: string; time: string }> = {
      fajr: { title: `اذان صبح (${city.nameFa})`, time: t.fajr },
      dhuhr: { title: `اذان ظهر (${city.nameFa})`, time: t.dhuhr },
      asr: { title: `وقت عصر (${city.nameFa})`, time: t.asr },
      maghrib: { title: `اذان مغرب (${city.nameFa})`, time: t.maghrib },
      isha: { title: `وقت عشاء (${city.nameFa})`, time: t.isha },
    };

    for (const [key, info] of Object.entries(prayerMap)) {
      const remId = `prayer_auto_${key}`;
      const existing = all.find((r) => r.id === remId);
      map[key] = Boolean(existing?.enabled);
      if (existing && existing.enabled && existing.time !== info.time) {
        const updated: LocalReminder = {
          ...existing,
          title: info.title,
          time: info.time,
          updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        };
        saveReminder(updated);
        void scheduleReminder(updated, true);
      }
    }
    setEnabledAlarms(map);
  }, [city, method, todayDate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (times) {
      const n = getNextPrayer(times, city.timezone);
      if (n) {
        const ordered = [
          { key: 'fajr', time: times.fajr },
          { key: 'dhuhr', time: times.dhuhr },
          { key: 'asr', time: times.asr },
          { key: 'maghrib', time: times.maghrib },
          { key: 'isha', time: times.isha },
        ];
        const index = ordered.findIndex((p) => p.key === n.key);
        const previous = ordered[(index - 1 + ordered.length) % ordered.length];
        const nextMinute = timeToMinutes(n.time);
        const previousMinute = timeToMinutes(previous.time);
        const clockParts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
          timeZone: city.timezone, hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hourCycle: 'h23',
        }).formatToParts(now);
        const hour = Number(clockParts.find((p) => p.type === 'hour')?.value ?? 0);
        const minute = Number(clockParts.find((p) => p.type === 'minute')?.value ?? 0);
        const nowMinute = hour * 60 + minute;
        let remaining = nextMinute - nowMinute;
        if (remaining < 0) remaining += 1440;
        let interval = nextMinute - previousMinute;
        if (interval <= 0) interval += 1440;
        const progress = Number.isFinite(interval) && interval > 0
          ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, ((interval - remaining) / interval) * 100))
          : 0;
        setNext({ key: n.key, nameFa: n.nameFa, time: n.time, remaining: n.remaining, progress });
        if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android' && typeof NoorAlarm.updatePrayerWidget === 'function') {
          void NoorAlarm.updatePrayerWidget({
            nextPrayerText: `اذان ${n.nameFa}: ساعت ${n.time}`,
            subtitleText: `به افق ${city.nameFa} (${n.remaining} مانده)`,
          });
        }
      }
    }
  }, [times, now, city.timezone]);

  const showToast = (msg: string) => {
    setStatusToast(msg);
    setTimeout(() => setStatusToast(null), 3200);
  };

  const detectNearestCity = async () => {
    const applyCoords = (latitude: number, longitude: number) => {
      let nearest = CITIES[0];
      let minDist = Infinity;
      for (const c of CITIES) {
        const d = Math.hypot(c.lat - latitude, c.lng - longitude);
        if (d < minDist) {
          minDist = d;
          nearest = c;
        }
      }
      if (profile) {
        void updateProfile({ city_id: nearest.id });
      } else {
        setGuestCityId(nearest.id);
        setGuestCityIdState(nearest.id);
      }
      showToast(`📍 موقعیت شما تشخیص داده شد: «${nearest.nameFa}»`);
    };

    showToast("در حال درخواست مجوز و تشخیص موقعیت مکانی...");
    if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === "android" && typeof NoorAlarm.requestAndGetLocation === "function") {
      try {
        const res = await NoorAlarm.requestAndGetLocation();
        if (res.hasCoords && typeof res.lat === "number" && typeof res.lng === "number") {
          applyCoords(res.lat, res.lng);
          return;
        }
      } catch {
        // در صورت نیاز ادامه به geolocation استاندارد پس از اخذ مجوز
      }
    }

    if ("geolocation" in navigator) {
      navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
        (pos) => applyCoords(pos.coords.latitude, pos.coords.longitude),
        () => showToast("لطفاً GPS گوشی را روشن کنید یا از تنظیمات شهر را انتخاب نمایید."),
        { enableHighAccuracy: false, timeout: 10000, maximumAge: 600000 }
      );
    } else {
      showToast("از بخش تنظیمات، شهر خود را از بین ۴۰ شهر انتخاب کنید.");
    }
  };

  const togglePrayerAdhanAlarm = async (key: string, nameFa: string, timeStrVal: string) => {
    if (key === 'sunrise') return;
    const remId = `prayer_auto_${key}`;
    const currentlyEnabled = Boolean(enabledAlarms[key]);
    const nextState = !currentlyEnabled;

    if (nextState) {
      let perm = await checkPermission();
      if (perm !== 'granted') {
        perm = await requestPermission();
      }
      if (perm !== 'granted') {
        showToast('لطفاً ابتدا مجوز اعلان را تایید کنید تا آلارم اذان فعال شود.');
        return;
      }
    }

    const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
    const reminder: LocalReminder = {
      id: remId,
      title: `اذان ${nameFa} به افق ${city.nameFa}`,
      body: `حَیَّ عَلَی الصَّلَاة — وقت فضیلت نماز ${nameFa} فرا رسیده است`,
      time: timeStrVal,
      days: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6],
      enabled: nextState,
      dhikrId: null,
      type: 'recurring',
      date: null,
      createdAt: nowIso,
      updatedAt: nowIso,
    };

    saveReminder(reminder);

    if (nextState) {
      const res = await scheduleReminder(reminder, true);
      logEvent(res.success ? 'info' : 'warn', 'HomeScreen', `تغییر وضعیت آلارم اذان ${nameFa}`, {
        enabled: true,
        time: timeStrVal,
        success: res.success,
        error: res.error,
      });
      if (res.success) {
        setEnabledAlarms((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: true }));
        showToast(`🔔 آلارم اذان ${nameFa} (${timeStrVal}) فعال شد`);
      } else {
        reminder.enabled = false;
        saveReminder(reminder);
        showToast(res.error ?? 'خطا در فعال‌سازی آلارم اذان');
      }
    } else {
      await cancelReminder(remId);
      logEvent('info', 'HomeScreen', `آلارم اذان ${nameFa} غیرفعال شد`);
      setEnabledAlarms((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: false }));
      showToast(`آلارم اذان ${nameFa} خاموش شد`);
    }
  };

  if (!times) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh]">
        <div className="animate-pulse-ring w-14 h-14 rounded-full" style={{ background: theme.primary, opacity: 0.3 }} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const prayers = [
    { key: 'fajr', nameFa: 'صبح', time: times.fajr },
    { key: 'sunrise', nameFa: 'طلوع', time: times.sunrise },
    { key: 'dhuhr', nameFa: 'ظهر', time: times.dhuhr },
    { key: 'asr', nameFa: 'عصر', time: times.asr },
    { key: 'maghrib', nameFa: 'مغرب', time: times.maghrib },
    { key: 'isha', nameFa: 'عشاء', time: times.isha },
  ];

  const timeStr = now.toLocaleTimeString('fa-IR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  const session = getSession();
  const activeDhikr = dhikrLibrary.find((d) => d.id === session.dhikrId);
  const HeroIcon = prayerIcons[next?.key ?? 'isha'] ?? Moon;
  const spiritualTip = getContextualSpiritualTip(now, next?.key);

  return (
    <div className="px-5 pt-6 pb-28 animate-fade-in">
      {showStudioModal && (
        <AdhanStudioAndTravelModal
          onClose={() => setShowStudioModal(false)}
          onChanged={() => setIntelTick((t) => t + 1)}
        />
      )}
      {showQuranModal && (
        <QuranAndMuhasabaModal
          initialMode="quran"
          onClose={() => setShowQuranModal(false)}
          onUpdated={() => setIntelTick((t) => t + 1)}
        />
      )}
      {showAnalyticsModal && (
        <VipAndAnalyticsModal onClose={() => setShowAnalyticsModal(false)} />
      )}
      {showCalendar && (
        <CalendarModal
          cityLat={city.lat}
          cityLng={city.lng}
          cityTimezone={city.timezone}
          cityName={city.nameFa}
          onClose={() => setShowCalendar(false)}
        />
      )}
      {showQibla && (
        <QiblaCompassModal
          cityName={city.nameFa}
          lat={city.lat}
          lng={city.lng}
          onClose={() => setShowQibla(false)}
        />
      )}

      {showTutorial && (
        <AppTutorialModal onClose={() => setShowTutorial(false)} />
      )}

      {statusToast && (
        <div
          className="fixed top-4 inset-x-5 z-50 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-center shadow-xl animate-fade-in"
          style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
        >
          {statusToast}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* هدر استاندارد، خلوت و متمرکز */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-5">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-app">
            {profile?.display_name ? profile.display_name : 'نور'}
          </h1>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowCalendar(true)} className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold" style={{ color: theme.primary }}><Calendar size={13} /><span>{getJalaliWeekday(now)}، {toJalaliShort(now)} (مشاهده تقویم)</span></button>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowQibla(true)}
            aria-label="قبله‌نما"
            className="px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1 border"
            style={{ borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.primary, background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 12%, transparent)` }}
          >
            <Compass size={14} />
            <span>قبله‌نما</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={detectNearestCity}
            aria-label="موقعیت"
            title="تشخیص خودکار شهر"
            className="px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium border"
            style={{ borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.text }}
          >
            <MapPin size={14} color={theme.primary} />
            <span>{times.location}</span>
            <LocateFixed size={11} className="opacity-60" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* کارت آسمان پویا بر اساس هر نماز (خورشید در روز، طلوع در فجر، غروب در مغرب و ماه در شب) */}
      <section
        className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[1.7rem] p-5 mb-4 animate-slide-up"
        aria-label="نماز بعدی"
        style={{
          background: `radial-gradient(circle at 90% 8%, color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.accent} 22%, transparent), transparent 42%), linear-gradient(145deg, ${theme.surfaceAlt ?? theme.surface}, ${theme.surface} 68%, ${theme.bg})`,
          border: `1px solid color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 28%, ${theme.border})`,
          boxShadow: `0 14px 34px color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 10%, transparent)`,
        }}
      >
        <div className="relative flex items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-5">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full animate-pulse" style={{ background: theme.accent }} />
              <p className="text-muted text-xs font-medium">{times.hijriDate} · ساعت {timeStr}</p>
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-primary">{next?.nameFa ?? '—'}</h2>
            <p className="text-muted text-xs mt-1">وقت شرعی بعدی به افق {times.location}</p>
          </div>
          <div
            className="w-[74px] h-[74px] rounded-[1.5rem] flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0"
            style={{
              background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 14%, transparent)`,
              border: `1px solid color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 24%, transparent)`,
            }}
          >
            <HeroIcon size={36} strokeWidth={1.6} color={theme.accent} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="relative flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3" style={{ background: 'color-mix(in srgb, black 12%, transparent)', border: `1px solid color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.border} 75%, transparent)` }}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
            <Clock size={16} color={theme.accent} />
            <p className="text-accent text-sm font-semibold truncate">{next?.remaining ? `تا اذان ${next.nameFa}: ${next.remaining}` : 'در حال محاسبه…'}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="text-left flex-shrink-0">
            <p className="text-muted text-[10px] mb-0.5">وقت اذان</p>
            <p className="text-lg font-bold text-app tabular-nums" dir="ltr">{next?.time ?? '--:--'}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="relative mt-3" aria-label="میزان نزدیک شدن به نماز بعدی">
          <div className="h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden" style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 12%, transparent)` }}>
            <div
              className="h-full rounded-full transition-[width] duration-1000 ease-linear"
              style={{ width: `${next?.progress ?? 0}%`, background: `linear-gradient(90deg, ${theme.primary}, ${theme.accent})` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* دستیار هوشمند نماز (Prayer Intelligence: پروفایل صدا، مسجد و ادامه قرآن) */}
      {(() => {
        void intelTick;
        const studio = loadAdhanStudio();
        const quran = loadQuranContinuity();
        const mt = loadMosqueAndTravel();
        const activeProf = studio.profiles[studio.activeProfile];
        const nextJamaat =
          next?.key === 'fajr' || next?.key === 'dhuhr' || next?.key === 'maghrib'
            ? addMinutesToTimeStr(next.time, mt.jamaatOffsetsMinutes[next.key])
            : null;

        return (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 mb-4">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowStudioModal(true)}
              className="card-flat p-3 text-right flex flex-col justify-between"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between w-full mb-1">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-primary">{activeProf.badge}</span>
                <Sliders size={13} color={theme.primary} />
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-app truncate">
                {mt.travelModeEnabled ? '✈️ حالت سفر (نماز شکسته)' : mt.selectedMosqueName}
              </p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-muted mt-0.5 truncate">
                {nextJamaat ? `جماعت ${next?.nameFa}: ${nextJamaat}` : 'تنظیم استودیو اذان و مسجد'}
              </p>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowQuranModal(true)}
              className="card-flat p-3 text-right flex flex-col justify-between"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between w-full mb-1">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-accent">📖 ادامه قرآن</span>
                <BookOpen size={13} color={theme.accent} />
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-app truncate">{quran.surahNameFa}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-muted mt-0.5 truncate">
                ادامه از بخش {quran.ayahNumber.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} (هدف {quran.dailyTargetMinutes.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}د)
              </p>
            </button>
          </div>
        );
      })()}

      {/* میان‌بر هوشمند پیشنهاد معنوی روز و ایام خاص */}
      <div
        className="card-flat p-4 mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 cursor-pointer"
        style={{ borderColor: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 30%, ${theme.border})` }}
        onClick={() => {
          const targetDhikr = dhikrLibrary.find((d) => d.id === spiritualTip.dhikrId);
          if (targetDhikr && onNavigate) {
            startSession(targetDhikr.id, targetDhikr.count);
            onNavigate('tasbih');
          }
        }}
      >
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1">
            <Sparkles size={13} color={theme.accent} />
            <span className="text-[11px] font-bold" style={{ color: theme.accent }}>{spiritualTip.badge}</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-app font-bold text-xs truncate">{spiritualTip.title}</p>
          <p className="text-muted text-[11px] mt-0.5 truncate">{spiritualTip.subtitle}</p>
        </div>
        <ArrowLeft size={18} color={theme.primary} className="shrink-0" />
      </div>

      {hasActiveSession && onNavigate && activeDhikr && (
        <button
          onClick={() => onNavigate('tasbih')}
          className="w-full card-flat p-4 mb-4 flex items-center justify-between animate-slide-up"
          aria-label="ادامه شمارش"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div
              className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0"
              style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 15%, transparent)` }}
            >
              <Hand size={20} color={theme.primary} />
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <p className="text-app font-bold text-sm">ادامه شمارش تسبیح</p>
              <p className="text-muted text-xs mt-0.5">
                {activeDhikr.arabic} — {session.count}/{session.target}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <ArrowLeft size={18} color={theme.primary} />
        </button>
      )}

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 px-1">
        <span className="text-muted text-[11px]">اوقات شرعی امروز</span>
        <span className="text-muted text-[11px]">لمس 🔔 = پخش خودکار اذان</span>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 mb-4">
        {prayers.map((p) => {
          const Icon = prayerIcons[p.key] ?? Sun;
          const isNext = next?.nameFa === p.nameFa && p.key !== 'sunrise';
          const isAlarmOn = Boolean(enabledAlarms[p.key]);
          const canAlarm = p.key !== 'sunrise';

          return (
            <div
              key={p.key}
              className="card-flat min-w-0 p-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-1.5"
              style={{ borderColor: isAlarmOn ? theme.primary : undefined }}
            >
              <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
                <div
                  className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl flex-shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center"
                  style={{ background: isNext ? theme.primary : `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 10%, transparent)` }}
                >
                  <Icon size={16} color={isNext ? theme.bg : theme.primary} />
                </div>
                <span className="text-app font-medium text-xs whitespace-nowrap">{p.nameFa}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-shrink-0">
                <span className="text-app font-bold text-xs tabular-nums" dir="ltr">{p.time}</span>
                {canAlarm && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={isAlarmOn ? `خاموش کردن آلارم اذان ${p.nameFa}` : `فعال کردن آلارم اذان ${p.nameFa}`}
                    onClick={() => void togglePrayerAdhanAlarm(p.key, p.nameFa, p.time)}
                    className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center transition-all"
                    style={{
                      background: isAlarmOn
                        ? theme.primary
                        : `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.textMuted} 15%, transparent)`,
                      color: isAlarmOn ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
                    }}
                  >
                    {isAlarmOn ? <Bell size={13} /> : <BellOff size={13} />}
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="card p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
          <h3 className="text-app font-bold text-xs">خلاصه فعالیت امروز</h3>
          {onNavigate && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onNavigate('settings')}
              className="text-[11px] font-bold text-primary"
            >
              مشاهده کارنامه کامل ←
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-center">
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{stats.total.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}</p>
            <p className="text-muted text-xs mt-0.5">ذکر امروز</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-accent">{stats.rounds.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}</p>
            <p className="text-muted text-xs mt-0.5">دور کامل</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/LibraryScreen.tsx

import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { BookOpen, Clock, Sparkles, BellPlus, Check, ArrowRight, Quote, Plus, Search, X, ScrollText, Star, Compass, HeartHandshake } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme } from '@/hooks/useTheme';
import { getAllDhikr, addCustomDhikr, dhikrCategories, type Dhikr } from '@/lib/dhikrLibrary';
import { createReminder } from '@/lib/reminderService';
import { startSession } from '@/lib/tasbihStorage';
import { toFaDigits } from '@/lib/jalali';
import type { Tab } from '@/components/BottomNav';

type MacroGroupKey = 'all' | 'scholars' | 'quran' | 'mafatih' | 'daily';

const MACRO_GROUPS: Array<{
  key: MacroGroupKey;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  icon: typeof Sparkles;
  match: (cat: string) => boolean;
}> = [
  {
    key: 'scholars',
    title: 'توصیه علما و بزرگان',
    subtitle: 'آیت‌الله فاطمی‌نیا، بهجت و بزرگان',
    icon: Star,
    match: (c) => c === 'scholars',
  },
  {
    key: 'quran',
    title: 'سوره‌ها و آیات مجرب',
    subtitle: 'واقعه، ذاریات و آیات گره‌گشا',
    icon: BookOpen,
    match: (c) => c === 'quran',
  },
  {
    key: 'mafatih',
    title: 'مفاتیح و زیارات',
    subtitle: 'شب جمعه، امام رضا (ع) و والدین',
    icon: Compass,
    match: (c) => c === 'mafatih' || c === 'salawat',
  },
  {
    key: 'daily',
    title: 'تعقیبات و اذکار روزانه',
    subtitle: 'تسبیحات، استغفار و توکل',
    icon: HeartHandshake,
    match: (c) => c === 'tasbih' || c === 'tahlil' || c === 'takbir' || c === 'istighfar',
  },
];

export function LibraryScreen({ onNavigate }: { onNavigate?: (t: Tab) => void }) {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const [items, setItems] = useState<Dhikr[]>(() => getAllDhikr());
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Dhikr | null>(null);
  const [activeGroup, setActiveGroup] = useState<MacroGroupKey>('all');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [reminderTime, setReminderTime] = useState('08:00');
  const [scheduleStatus, setScheduleStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showAddCustom, setShowAddCustom] = useState(false);
  const [customArabic, setCustomArabic] = useState('');
  const [customTranslation, setCustomTranslation] = useState('');
  const [customSource, setCustomSource] = useState('');
  const [customTimeNote, setCustomTimeNote] = useState('');
  const [customCount, setCustomCount] = useState('14');

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    return items.filter((d) => {
      if (activeGroup !== 'all') {
        const grp = MACRO_GROUPS.find((g) => g.key === activeGroup);
        if (grp && !grp.match(d.category)) return false;
      }
      if (!q) return true;
      return (
        d.arabic.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        d.translation.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (d.source ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (d.recommendedTime ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (d.scholarNote ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    });
  }, [items, activeGroup, searchQuery]);

  const handleOpenDhikr = (d: Dhikr) => {
    setSelected(d);
    setReminderTime(d.defaultReminderTime ?? '08:00');
    setScheduleStatus(null);
  };

  const handleStartTasbih = (d: Dhikr) => {
    startSession(d.id, d.count);
    onNavigate?.('tasbih');
  };

  const handleAddToMyPlan = async (d: Dhikr) => {
    setScheduleStatus('در حال ثبت در برنامه روزانه شما...');
    const res = await createReminder({
      title: `برنامه معنوی: ${d.arabic.slice(0, 38)}`,
      body: `${d.translation} (${toFaDigits(d.count)} مرتبه)`,
      time: reminderTime,
      days: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6],
      dhikrId: d.id,
      type: 'recurring',
      notifEnabled: true,
    });
    if (res.schedule.success) {
      setScheduleStatus(`✅ در برنامه روزانه شما برای ساعت ${toFaDigits(reminderTime)} ثبت شد.`);
    } else {
      setScheduleStatus(`⚠️ در لیست برنامه ذخیره شد (${res.schedule.error ?? 'مجوز اعلان را در تنظیمات بررسی کنید'}).`);
    }
  };

  const handleCreateCustomNote = () => {
    if (!customArabic.trim()) return;
    const item = addCustomDhikr({
      arabic: customArabic,
      translation: customTranslation,
      count: Number(customCount) || 1,
      source: customSource,
      recommendedTime: customTimeNote,
    });
    setItems(getAllDhikr());
    setShowAddCustom(false);
    setCustomArabic('');
    setCustomTranslation('');
    setCustomSource('');
    setCustomTimeNote('');
    setSelected(item);
  };

  if (selected) {
    return (
      <div className="px-5 pt-6 pb-28 animate-fade-in" dir="rtl">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setSelected(null)}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted text-sm font-bold mb-4"
        >
          <ArrowRight size={17} />
          <span>بازگشت به گنجینه اذکار</span>
        </button>

        <div
          className="rounded-3xl p-6 mb-4 relative overflow-hidden border"
          style={{
            background: `radial-gradient(circle at top right, color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 16%, transparent), ${theme.surface})`,
            borderColor: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 35%, ${theme.border})`,
          }}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
            <span
              className="text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full"
              style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 15%, transparent)`, color: theme.primary }}
            >
              {dhikrCategories[selected.category] ?? 'ذکر'}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary">
              تعداد مجرب: {toFaDigits(selected.count)} مرتبه
            </span>
          </div>

          <p className="text-app text-2xl font-bold leading-loose text-center my-4">
            {selected.arabic}
          </p>
          <p className="text-app text-sm leading-relaxed text-center font-medium mb-4">
            {selected.translation}
          </p>

          {selected.source && (
            <div className="text-center text-xs text-muted mb-4">
              📚 سند و منبع: <strong className="text-app">{selected.source}</strong>
            </div>
          )}

          {selected.recommendedTime && (
            <div
              className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl mb-3 flex items-start gap-2.5"
              style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.accent} 12%, transparent)` }}
            >
              <Clock size={16} color={theme.accent} className="shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <div className="text-xs leading-relaxed">
                <span className="font-bold text-app">زمان مجرب خواندن: </span>
                <span className="text-muted">{selected.recommendedTime}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div
            className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl mb-3"
            style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 10%, transparent)` }}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-primary mb-1">
              <Sparkles size={14} />
              <span>فضیلت و برکت</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-app text-xs leading-relaxed">{selected.virtue}</p>
          </div>

          {selected.hadith && (
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl border mb-3" style={{ borderColor: theme.border }}>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold mb-1" style={{ color: theme.accent }}>
                <Quote size={14} />
                <span>کلام معصوم (ع)</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-muted text-xs leading-relaxed">{selected.hadith}</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {selected.scholarNote && (
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl border" style={{ borderColor: theme.border }}>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-primary mb-1">🌿 توصیه علما و بزرگان</p>
              <p className="text-muted text-xs leading-relaxed">{selected.scholarNote}</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="card-flat p-4 mb-4 space-y-3">
          <p className="text-app font-bold text-sm">ثبت مستقیم در برنامه یادآوری روزانه</p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <input
              type="time"
              value={reminderTime}
              onChange={(e) => setReminderTime(e.target.value)}
              dir="ltr"
              className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm text-app border outline-none"
              style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => void handleAddToMyPlan(selected)}
              className="flex-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
              style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 16%, transparent)`, color: theme.primary }}
            >
              <BellPlus size={15} />
              <span>افزودن به برنامه من</span>
            </button>
          </div>
          {scheduleStatus && (
            <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-primary">{scheduleStatus}</p>
          )}
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => handleStartTasbih(selected)}
          className="w-full py-4 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg"
          style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
        >
          <Check size={18} />
          <span>شروع شمارش در تسبیح ({toFaDigits(selected.count)} مرتبه)</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="px-5 pt-6 pb-28 animate-fade-in" dir="rtl">
      {/* مودال مرکزچین با Portal روی document.body */}
      {showAddCustom && typeof document !== 'undefined' && createPortal(
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-[999] overflow-y-auto flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-fade-in"
          style={{ background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.78)', backdropFilter: 'blur(6px)' }}
          dir="rtl"
        >
          <div
            className="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-5 border shadow-2xl space-y-3"
            style={{ background: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border }}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-app">ثبت نکته سخنرانی یا ذکر شخصی</h3>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowAddCustom(false)} className="text-muted">
                <X size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>
            <input
              value={customArabic}
              onChange={(e) => setCustomArabic(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="متن ذکر، آیه یا عمل (مثلاً: سوره واقعه یا صدقه)"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-3 rounded-2xl text-sm text-app border outline-none"
              style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
            />
            <input
              value={customTranslation}
              onChange={(e) => setCustomTranslation(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="ترجمه، نیت یا توضیح (مثلاً: هدیه به والدین)"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-3 rounded-2xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
              style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
            />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <input
                value={customSource}
                onChange={(e) => setCustomSource(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="گوینده / منبع (مثلاً: استاد عالی)"
                className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
                style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
              />
              <input
                value={customTimeNote}
                onChange={(e) => setCustomTimeNote(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="زمان (مثلاً: بعد نماز صبح)"
                className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
                style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="text-xs text-muted">تعداد:</span>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="1"
                  value={customCount}
                  onChange={(e) => setCustomCount(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-20 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs text-app border outline-none text-center"
                  style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
                  dir="ltr"
                />
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleCreateCustomNote}
                className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold"
                style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
              >
                ذخیره در گنجینه من
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>,
        document.body
      )}

      {/* هدر استاندارد */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-app">گنجینه اذکار و ادعیه</h1>
          <p className="text-muted text-xs mt-1">قرآن، مفاتیح، صحیفه سجادیه و توصیه علما</p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setShowAddCustom(true)}
          className="px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 shadow-sm"
          style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
        >
          <Plus size={15} />
          <span>یادداشت سخنرانی</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* نوار جستجوی سریع و مدرن */}
      <div
        className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl border mb-4"
        style={{ background: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border }}
      >
        <Search size={17} color={theme.textMuted} className="shrink-0" />
        <input
          type="text"
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="جستجو در نام ذکر، آیت‌الله فاطمی‌نیا، واقعه، امام رضا..."
          className="w-full bg-transparent text-xs text-app outline-none"
        />
        {searchQuery && (
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')} className="text-muted">
            <X size={15} />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* کارت‌های Bento Grid دسته‌بندی (کاملاً ریسپانسیو، بدون هیچ تداخل متنی) */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 mb-4">
        {MACRO_GROUPS.map((grp) => {
          const Icon = grp.icon;
          const isSelected = activeGroup === grp.key;
          const count = items.filter((i) => grp.match(i.category)).length;
          return (
            <button
              key={grp.key}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveGroup(isSelected ? 'all' : grp.key)}
              className="card-flat p-3.5 text-right transition-all flex flex-col justify-between"
              style={{
                borderColor: isSelected ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                background: isSelected
                  ? `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 14%, ${theme.surface})`
                  : theme.surface,
              }}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between w-full mb-2">
                <div
                  className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center"
                  style={{
                    background: isSelected ? theme.primary : `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 12%, transparent)`,
                  }}
                >
                  <Icon size={16} color={isSelected ? theme.bg : theme.primary} />
                </div>
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-primary">
                  {toFaDigits(count)} مورد
                </span>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-app font-bold text-xs">{grp.title}</p>
                <p className="text-muted text-[10px] mt-0.5 truncate">{grp.subtitle}</p>
              </div>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {activeGroup !== 'all' && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 px-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary">
            فیلتر فعال: {MACRO_GROUPS.find((g) => g.key === activeGroup)?.title}
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveGroup('all')}
            className="text-xs text-muted underline"
          >
            نمایش همه ({toFaDigits(items.length)})
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* لیست کارت‌های ذکر با تایپوگرافی استاندارد و تمیز */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {filtered.map((d) => (
          <button
            key={d.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleOpenDhikr(d)}
            className="w-full card-flat p-4 text-right transition-transform active:scale-[0.99]"
          >
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-2">
              <p className="text-app font-bold text-base leading-relaxed flex-1">{d.arabic}</p>
              <span
                className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl shrink-0"
                style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 12%, transparent)`, color: theme.primary }}
              >
                {dhikrCategories[d.category]}
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-muted text-xs leading-relaxed">{d.translation}</p>
            <div
              className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mt-3 pt-2.5"
              style={{ borderTop: `1px solid ${theme.border}` }}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted text-[11px] min-w-0">
                <ScrollText size={12} color={theme.primary} className="shrink-0" />
                <span className="truncate">{d.source ?? 'مفاتیح‌الجنان'}</span>
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary shrink-0">
                {toFaDigits(d.count)} مرتبه
              </span>
            </div>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/NotificationPermissionModal.tsx
========================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Bell, X, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { Capacitor } from '@capacitor/core';
import { LocalNotifications } from '@capacitor/local-notifications';
import { useTheme } from '@/hooks/useTheme';
import { checkPermission, requestPermission, rescheduleAll } from '@/lib/reminderService';
import { logEvent } from '@/lib/logger';

const ONBOARDING_PROMPT_KEY = 'noor_notif_onboarding_prompted_v1';
const GUEST_NOTIF_KEY = 'noor_guest_notif_enabled';

interface Props {
  onGranted?: () => void;
}

export function NotificationPermissionModal({ onGranted }: Props) {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;

    const checkInitialPrompt = async () => {
      const isNative = Capacitor.isNativePlatform();
      const alreadyPrompted = localStorage.getItem(ONBOARDING_PROMPT_KEY) === 'true';
      const perm = await checkPermission();

      logEvent('info', 'OnboardingModal', 'بررسی اولیه وضعیت مجوز در بدو ورود', {
        isNative,
        alreadyPrompted,
        permission: perm,
      });

      if (perm === 'granted') {
        localStorage.setItem(ONBOARDING_PROMPT_KEY, 'true');
        return;
      }

      if (!alreadyPrompted && (isNative || (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'Notification' in window))) {
        timer = setTimeout(() => {
          setVisible(true);
          logEvent('info', 'OnboardingModal', 'مودال معرفی مجوز اعلان به کاربر نمایش داده شد');
        }, 900);
      }
    };

    void checkInitialPrompt();
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, []);

  const handleAllow = async () => {
    setSubmitting(true);
    logEvent('info', 'OnboardingModal', 'کاربر روی دکمه فعال کردن یادآوری‌ها کلیک کرد');

    const result = await requestPermission();
    localStorage.setItem(ONBOARDING_PROMPT_KEY, 'true');

    logEvent(result === 'granted' ? 'info' : 'warn', 'OnboardingModal', 'پاسخ سیستم به درخواست مجوز اعلان', {
      result,
    });

    if (result === 'granted') {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(GUEST_NOTIF_KEY, 'true');
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }

      if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android') {
        try {
          const exact = await LocalNotifications.checkExactNotificationSetting();
          logEvent('info', 'OnboardingModal', 'وضعیت مجوز Exact Alarm بررسی شد', {
            exact_alarm: exact.exact_alarm,
          });
        } catch (err) {
          logEvent('warn', 'OnboardingModal', 'خطا در بررسی Exact Alarm', { error: String(err) });
        }
      }

      await rescheduleAll(true);
      onGranted?.();
    }

    setSubmitting(false);
    setVisible(false);
  };

  const handleDismiss = () => {
    localStorage.setItem(ONBOARDING_PROMPT_KEY, 'true');
    logEvent('info', 'OnboardingModal', 'کاربر گزینه فعلاً نه را انتخاب کرد');
    setVisible(false);
  };

  if (!visible) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-5 animate-fade-in"
      style={{ background: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65)', backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)' }}
    >
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl p-6 text-center relative shadow-2xl border"
        style={{ background: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border }}
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleDismiss}
          aria-label="بستن"
          className="absolute top-4 left-4 text-muted"
        >
          <X size={20} />
        </button>

        <div
          className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl mx-auto mb-4 flex items-center justify-center"
          style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 15%, transparent)` }}
        >
          <Bell size={30} color={theme.primary} />
        </div>

        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-app mb-2">
          یادت نره ذکر امروزت رو بگی! 🌿
        </h2>

        <p className="text-muted text-xs leading-relaxed mb-6">
          با فعال کردن یادآوری‌ها، برنامهٔ «نور» در زمان مناسب بهت یادآوری می‌کنه که چند لحظه برای آرامش، ذکر و نماز وقت بذاری.
        </p>

        <div className="space-y-2.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={submitting}
            onClick={handleAllow}
            className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
            style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
          >
            <Check size={18} />
            {submitting ? 'در حال دریافت مجوز...' : 'فعال کردن یادآوری‌ها'}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={submitting}
            onClick={handleDismiss}
            className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs font-medium text-muted"
          >
            فعلاً نه، بعداً از تنظیمات فعال می‌کنم
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src/components/QiblaCompassModal.tsx
========================================
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Compass, X, Navigation, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme } from '@/hooks/useTheme';
import { toFaDigits } from '@/lib/jalali';

interface Props {
  cityName: string;
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const KAABA_LAT = 21.422487;
const KAABA_LNG = 39.826206;

export function calculateQiblaAngle(lat: number, lng: number): number {
  const toRad = (d: number) => (d * Math.PI) / 180;
  const toDeg = (r: number) => (r * 180) / Math.PI;

  const phiK = toRad(KAABA_LAT);
  const lambdaK = toRad(KAABA_LNG);
  const phi = toRad(lat);
  const lambda = toRad(lng);

  const y = Math.sin(lambdaK - lambda);
  const x = Math.cos(phi) * Math.tan(phiK) - Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(lambdaK - lambda);
  const qibla = toDeg(Math.atan2(y, x));
  return (qibla + 360) % 360;
}

function shortestAngleDiff(a: number, b: number): number {
  const diff = ((a - b + 540) % 360) - 180;
  return Math.abs(diff);
}

export function QiblaCompassModal({ cityName, lat, lng, onClose }: Props) {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const [smoothHeading, setSmoothHeading] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const sinRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const cosRef = useRef<number>(1);
  const initializedRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const lastCommittedHeadingRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const lastEmitRef = useRef<number>(0);

  const qiblaAngle = Math.round(calculateQiblaAngle(lat, lng));

  useEffect(() => {
    // ضریب نرم‌کنندگی بالا + آستانه سکون ۱.۸ درجه برای حذف ۱۰۰٪ لرزش ریز دست
    const ALPHA = 0.06;
    const DEADBAND_DEG = 1.8;

    const handleOrientation = (e: DeviceOrientationEvent) => {
      const webkitHeading = (e as unknown as { webkitCompassHeading?: number }).webkitCompassHeading;
      let raw: number | null = null;
      if (typeof webkitHeading === 'number' && !isNaN(webkitHeading)) {
        raw = webkitHeading;
      } else if (typeof e.alpha === 'number' && !isNaN(e.alpha)) {
        raw = (360 - e.alpha) % 360;
      }
      if (raw === null) return;

      const rad = (raw * Math.PI) / 180;
      if (!initializedRef.current) {
        sinRef.current = Math.sin(rad);
        cosRef.current = Math.cos(rad);
        initializedRef.current = true;
      } else {
        sinRef.current = sinRef.current * (1 - ALPHA) + Math.sin(rad) * ALPHA;
        cosRef.current = cosRef.current * (1 - ALPHA) + Math.cos(rad) * ALPHA;
      }

      const now = Date.now();
      if (now - lastEmitRef.current > 80) {
        const filteredDeg = ((Math.atan2(sinRef.current, cosRef.current) * 180) / Math.PI + 360) % 360;
        const prev = lastCommittedHeadingRef.current;
        if (prev === null || shortestAngleDiff(filteredDeg, prev) >= DEADBAND_DEG) {
          lastEmitRef.current = now;
          lastCommittedHeadingRef.current = filteredDeg;
          setSmoothHeading(Math.round(filteredDeg));
        }
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('deviceorientationabsolute' as keyof WindowEventMap, handleOrientation as EventListener, true);
    window.addEventListener('deviceorientation', handleOrientation, true);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('deviceorientationabsolute' as keyof WindowEventMap, handleOrientation as EventListener, true);
      window.removeEventListener('deviceorientation', handleOrientation, true);
    };
  }, []);

  const relativeRotation = smoothHeading !== null ? (qiblaAngle - smoothHeading + 360) % 360 : qiblaAngle;
  const isAligned = smoothHeading !== null && (relativeRotation <= 7 || relativeRotation >= 353);

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-5 animate-fade-in"
      style={{ background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.72)', backdropFilter: 'blur(6px)' }}
      dir="rtl"
    >
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl p-6 text-center relative border shadow-2xl"
        style={{
          background: theme.surface,
          borderColor: isAligned ? theme.primary : theme.border,
        }}
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="بستن قبله‌نما"
          className="absolute top-4 left-4 text-muted"
        >
          <X size={22} />
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-1">
          <Compass size={20} color={theme.primary} />
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-app">قبله‌نمای دقیق</h2>
        </div>
        <p className="text-muted text-xs mb-5">
          افق {cityName} — زاویه قبله از شمال: <strong>{toFaDigits(qiblaAngle)}°</strong>
        </p>

        <div
          className="w-60 h-60 rounded-full mx-auto mb-5 relative flex items-center justify-center border-2"
          style={{
            borderColor: isAligned ? theme.primary : theme.border,
            background: `radial-gradient(circle, color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 15%, transparent), transparent 72%)`,
          }}
        >
          <div
            className="absolute -top-4 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold shadow-md flex items-center gap-1"
            style={{ background: isAligned ? theme.primary : theme.surface, color: isAligned ? theme.bg : theme.text, border: `1px solid ${theme.border}` }}
          >
            <span>🕋</span>
            <span>کعبه</span>
          </div>

          <span className="absolute bottom-2 text-[10px] text-muted">جنوب</span>
          <span className="absolute right-3 text-[10px] text-muted">شرق</span>
          <span className="absolute left-3 text-[10px] text-muted">غرب</span>

          <div
            className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center"
            style={{
              transform: `rotate(${relativeRotation}deg)`,
              transition: 'transform 220ms ease-out',
            }}
          >
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center -mt-14">
              <div
                className="w-9 h-9 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center shadow-md mb-1"
                style={{ background: isAligned ? theme.primary : theme.accent, color: theme.bg }}
              >
                🕋
              </div>
              <Navigation size={32} color={isAligned ? theme.primary : theme.accent} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isAligned ? (
          <div
            className="p-3 rounded-2xl mb-4 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold"
            style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 18%, transparent)`, color: theme.primary }}
          >
            <CheckCircle2 size={16} />
            <span>دقیقاً رو به قبله قرار دارید ✨</span>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-muted text-xs leading-relaxed mb-4">
            {smoothHeading !== null
              ? `گوشی را افقی بچرخانید تا نشانگر 🕋 چرخان با کعبه بالای صفحه منطبق شود.`
              : `زاویه قبله در ${cityName} برابر ${toFaDigits(qiblaAngle)} درجه در جهت عقربه‌های ساعت از شمال است.`}
          </p>
        )}

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="w-full py-3 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold"
          style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
        >
          بستن قبله‌نما
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src/components/QuranAndMuhasabaModal.tsx
========================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { BookOpen, Heart, Bookmark, ChevronRight, ChevronLeft, Check, X, Sparkles, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme } from '@/hooks/useTheme';
import {
  SELECTED_QURAN_PASSAGES,
  loadQuranContinuity,
  saveQuranContinuity,
  loadMuhasabaEntries,
  addMuhasabaEntry,
  type MuhasabaEntry,
} from '@/lib/deenIntelligenceStore';
import { toFaDigits, toJalaliShort } from '@/lib/jalali';

interface Props {
  initialMode?: 'quran' | 'muhasaba';
  onClose: () => void;
  onUpdated?: () => void;
}

export function QuranAndMuhasabaModal({ initialMode = 'quran', onClose, onUpdated }: Props) {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'quran' | 'muhasaba'>(initialMode);
  const [qState, setQState] = useState(() => loadQuranContinuity());
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState(() => loadMuhasabaEntries());
  const [mood, setMood] = useState<MuhasabaEntry['mood']>('peaceful');
  const [gratitude, setGratitude] = useState('');
  const [reflection, setReflection] = useState('');
  const [tomorrow, setTomorrow] = useState('');
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const currentSurah =
    SELECTED_QURAN_PASSAGES.find((s) => s.surahIndex === qState.surahIndex) ?? SELECTED_QURAN_PASSAGES[0];
  const currentAyahIndex = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(currentSurah.ayahs.length - 1, qState.ayahNumber - 1)
  );
  const currentAyah = currentSurah.ayahs[currentAyahIndex];

  const showToast = (m: string) => {
    setToast(m);
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2600);
  };

  const updateQuranPosition = (surahIndex: number, ayahNumber: number, addMinutes: number = 0) => {
    const surah = SELECTED_QURAN_PASSAGES.find((s) => s.surahIndex === surahIndex) ?? SELECTED_QURAN_PASSAGES[0];
    const next = {
      ...qState,
      surahIndex: surah.surahIndex,
      surahNameFa: surah.surahNameFa,
      ayahNumber,
      totalAyahs: surah.ayahs.length,
      todayReadMinutes: qState.todayReadMinutes + addMinutes,
      lastReadIso: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    setQState(next);
    saveQuranContinuity(next);
    onUpdated?.();
  };

  const handleBookmark = () => {
    const exists = qState.bookmarks.some(
      (b) => b.surahNameFa === currentSurah.surahNameFa && b.ayahNumber === currentAyah.number
    );
    if (exists) {
      showToast('این آیه قبلاً در نشانک‌های شما ذخیره شده است.');
      return;
    }
    const next = {
      ...qState,
      bookmarks: [
        { surahNameFa: currentSurah.surahNameFa, ayahNumber: currentAyah.number, note: currentAyah.translationFa.slice(0, 45) },
        ...qState.bookmarks,
      ].slice(0, 20),
    };
    setQState(next);
    saveQuranContinuity(next);
    showToast('🔖 آیه در نشانک‌های قرآنی شما ذخیره شد.');
    onUpdated?.();
  };

  const handleSaveMuhasaba = () => {
    if (!gratitude.trim() && !reflection.trim()) {
      showToast('لطفاً حداقل یک جمله شکرگزاری یا یادداشت وارد کنید.');
      return;
    }
    addMuhasabaEntry({
      date: toJalaliShort(new Date()),
      mood,
      gratitudeNote: gratitude.trim() || 'شکر نعمت سلامتی و فرصت بندگی',
      selfReflection: reflection.trim() || 'مراقبه بر آرامش و خوش‌خلقی با خانواده',
      tomorrowIntention: tomorrow.trim() || 'نماز اول وقت و قرائت قرآن',
    });
    setEntries(loadMuhasabaEntries());
    setGratitude('');
    setReflection('');
    setTomorrow('');
    showToast('✨ یادداشت محاسبه نفس به‌صورت کاملاً خصوصی روی گوشی ذخیره شد.');
    onUpdated?.();
  };

  const node = (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[999] overflow-y-auto flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-fade-in"
      style={{ background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.82)', backdropFilter: 'blur(6px)' }}
      dir="rtl"
    >
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-5 border shadow-2xl my-auto max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto"
        style={{ background: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border }}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-3 border-b" style={{ borderColor: theme.border }}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <BookOpen size={19} color={theme.primary} />
            <h2 className="text-base font-black text-app">انس مستمر با قرآن و محاسبه نفس</h2>
          </div>
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="text-muted p-1">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {toast && (
          <div
            className="mb-3 rounded-2xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-center"
            style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
          >
            {toast}
          </div>
        )}

        <div
          className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 p-1 rounded-2xl mb-4"
          style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 10%, transparent)` }}
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMode('quran')}
            className="py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
            style={{
              background: mode === 'quran' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'quran' ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
            }}
          >
            <BookOpen size={14} /> ادامه مطالعه قرآن
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMode('muhasaba')}
            className="py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
            style={{
              background: mode === 'muhasaba' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'muhasaba' ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
            }}
          >
            <Heart size={14} /> محاسبه نفس شبانه
          </button>
        </div>

        {mode === 'quran' ? (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 card-flat p-3">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-app">برنامه مطالعه روزانه:</span>
              <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                {([5, 15, 30] as const).map((min) => (
                  <button
                    key={min}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      const next = { ...qState, dailyTargetMinutes: min };
                      setQState(next);
                      saveQuranContinuity(next);
                    }}
                    className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold"
                    style={{
                      background: qState.dailyTargetMinutes === min ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
                      color: qState.dailyTargetMinutes === min ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
                      border: `1px solid ${qState.dailyTargetMinutes === min ? theme.primary : theme.border}`,
                    }}
                  >
                    {toFaDigits(min)} دقیقه
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              {SELECTED_QURAN_PASSAGES.map((s) => (
                <button
                  key={s.surahIndex}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => updateQuranPosition(s.surahIndex, 1, 0)}
                  className="p-2.5 rounded-xl text-right border text-xs font-bold"
                  style={{
                    borderColor: currentSurah.surahIndex === s.surahIndex ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                    background:
                      currentSurah.surahIndex === s.surahIndex
                        ? `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 14%, transparent)`
                        : 'transparent',
                    color: currentSurah.surahIndex === s.surahIndex ? theme.primary : theme.text,
                  }}
                >
                  {s.surahNameFa}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div
              className="rounded-3xl p-5 border text-center space-y-3"
              style={{
                background: `radial-gradient(circle at top, color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.primary} 18%, transparent), ${theme.bg})`,
                borderColor: theme.primary,
              }}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-muted">
                <span className="font-bold text-primary">{currentSurah.surahNameFa}</span>
                <span>
                  بخش {toFaDigits(currentAyahIndex + 1)} از {toFaDigits(currentSurah.ayahs.length)}
                </span>
              </div>

              <p className="text-xl font-bold text-app leading-loose py-2">{currentAyah.arabic}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted leading-relaxed">{currentAyah.translationFa}</p>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-3 border-t" style={{ borderColor: theme.border }}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={currentAyahIndex === 0}
                  onClick={() => updateQuranPosition(currentSurah.surahIndex, currentAyahIndex)}
                  className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1 border disabled:opacity-40"
                  style={{ borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.text }}
                >
                  <ChevronRight size={14} /> آیه قبل
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleBookmark}
                  className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1"
                  style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${theme.accent} 16%, transparent)`, color: theme.accent }}
                >
                  <Bookmark size={14} /> نشانک‌گذاری
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    const nextNum =
                      currentAyahIndex + 1 < currentSurah.ayahs.length ? currentAyahIndex + 2 : 1;
                    updateQuranPosition(currentSurah.surahIndex, nextNum, 2);
                    showToast('✓ موقعیت مطالعه ذخیره شد (ادامه از همین‌جا)');
                  }}
                  className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1"
                  style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
                >
                  <span>آیه بعد (ثبت)</span>
                  <ChevronLeft size={14} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3.5">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-muted">
              <Lock size={13} color={theme.primary} />
              <span>یادداشت‌های محاسبه نفس ۱۰۰٪ خصوصی و فقط روی حافظهٔ همین گوشی ذخیره می‌شوند.</span>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
              {([
                { id: 'peaceful' as const, label: '🌿 آرام' },
                { id: 'grateful' as const, label: '🤲 شاکر' },
                { id: 'hopeful' as const, label: '✨ امیدوار' },
                { id: 'repentant' as const, label: '💧 توبه‌کار' },
              ]).map((m) => (
                <button
                  key={m.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setMood(m.id)}
                  className="py-2 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold border"
                  style={{
                    borderColor: mood === m.id ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                    background: mood === m.id ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
                    color: mood === m.id ? theme.bg : theme.textMuted,
                  }}
                >
                  {m.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <input
              value={gratitude}
              onChange={(e) => setGratitude(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="۱. امروز بابت چه نعمتی خدا را شکر می‌کنی؟"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
              style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
            />
            <input
              value={reflection}
              onChange={(e) => setReflection(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="۲. محاسبه نفس امروز (قدم مثبت یا نکته‌ای که آموختم)"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
              style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
            />
            <input
              value={tomorrow}
              onChange={(e) => setTomorrow(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="۳. نیت و تصمیم معنوی من برای فردا"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs text-app border outline-none"
              style={{ borderColor: theme.border, background: theme.bg }}
            />

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSaveMuhasaba}
              className="w-full py-3 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
              style={{ background: theme.primary, color: theme.bg }}
            >
              <Sparkles size={15} /> ثبت در دفترچه خلوت من
            </button>

            {entries.length > 0 && (
              <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t max-h-40 overflow-y-auto" style={{ borderColor: theme.border }}>
                <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-muted">مرور یادداشت‌های قبلی من:</p>
                {entries.slice(0, 5).map((ent) => (
                  <div key={ent.id} className="card-flat p-2.5 text-xs space-y-1">
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-[10px] text-primary font-bold">
                      <span>{ent.date}</span>
                      <span>ثبت محلی ✓</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-app font-medium">🤲 {ent.gratitudeNote}</p>
                    <p className="text-muted text-[11px]">🌱 نیت فردا: {ent.tomorrowIntention}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="w-full mt-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold border"
          style={{ borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.text }}
        >
          <Check size={14} className="inline ml-1" /> بستن
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return typeof document !== 'undefined' ? createPortal(node, document.body) : node;
}

FILE: src/components/RemindersScreen.tsx

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

  loadReminders,
  createReminder,
  updateReminder,
  toggleReminder,
  deleteReminder,
  rescheduleAll,
  checkPermission,

  toggleReminderCompletionOnDate,

const GUEST_NOTIF_KEY = 'noor_guest_notif_enabled';

function getGuestNotifEnabled(): boolean {
  try {

    return true;
  }
}

const SMART_PRESETS: Array<{
  id: string;
  badge: string;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  time: string;
  days: number[];
  dhikrId: string | null;
  durationDays: number | null;
}> = [

    id: 'preset_parents',

    title: 'فاتحه و خیرات برای پدر، مادر و اهل قبور',
    body: 'خواندن سوره حمد، ۳ توحید و ۷ قدر برای شادی روح والدین و درگذشتگان',
    time: '16:30',

    dhikrId: 'ahlul_qubur_fatiha',
